2026年 10月 30日 全7頁

2026年 7-9月期 GDP（1次速報）予測 
～前期比年率＋1.5％を予想～ 
個人消費は小幅減だった一方、設備投資と輸出が実質GDPを押し上げ

経済調査部 エコノミスト 畑中 宏仁 
  チーフエコノミスト 神田 慶司

[要約] 
◼ 11月 16日公表予定の 2026年 7-9月期の GDP1次速報では、実質 GDPが前期比年率＋
1.5％（前期比＋0.4％）と、4四半期連続のプラス成長を予想する。個人消費は小幅
に減少したが、設備投資と輸出の増加が実質GDPを押し上げたようだ。 
◼ 個人消費はサービスが増加したものの、財が減少した影響の方が大きかったと予想す
る。設備投資は 4-6 月期に特殊要因で減少していた反動もあって増加したとみられる。
公共投資は減少、政府消費は増加したと見込んでいる。 
◼ 輸出と輸入はサービスが減少した一方、財が増加したと予想する。代替調達の進展に
よる鉱物性燃料輸入増加は実質 GDPを押し下げたが、石油国家備蓄の大規模放出が行
われなかったことで公的在庫変動が実質 GDP成長率を押し上げた分が相殺したようだ。

図表 1：2026年 7-9月期 GDP予測表

1．2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDP成長率は前期比年率＋1.5％を予想 
11月 16日公表予定の 2026年 7-9月期の GDP1次速報では、実質 GDPが前期比年率＋1.5％
（前期比＋0.4％）と、4四半期連続のプラス成長を予想する（10月30日正午までに公表された
基礎統計に基づく）。個人消費は小幅に減少したが、設備投資と輸出が実質GDPを押し上げたよ
うだ。 
個人消費はサービスが増加したものの、財が減少した影響の方が大きかったと予想する。設
備投資は4-6月期に特殊要因で減少していた反動もあって増加したとみられる。 
輸出と輸入はサービスが減少したものの、財が増加したと予想する。中東産原油の代替調達
の進展による鉱物性燃料輸入の増加は実質 GDPを押し下げたが、石油国家備蓄の大規模放出が
行われなかったことで公的在庫変動が実質GDP成長率を押し上げた分で相殺されたようだ。

図表 2：実質 GDP成長率と需要項目別寄与度の推移

民需：個人消費、住宅投資は減少の一方、設備投資は大幅な増加を予想 
個人消費は前期比▲0.1％と予想する。4-6月期のインバウンド（個人消費の控除項目）の実
績が上方改定されるであろうことを踏まえると、2 四半期連続のマイナスとなると見込んでい
る。サービスは増加したものの、耐久財と半耐久財、非耐久財がそろって減少したとみている。
耐久財では、自動車税の環境性能割廃止やエアコンの省エネ基準の引き上げの影響もあって 4-
6 月期に増加していた自動車や家電への支出が減少したようだ。半耐久財・非耐久財では衣服
やたばこ、ガソリンなどが増加したものの、食料品などが減少したとみられる。サービスは外
食や鉄道旅客、娯楽などが増加したようだ。 
住宅投資は前期比▲1.8％と予想する。国土交通省によると、7月の建設工事費デフレーター
（住宅総合、大和総研による季節調整値）が前月比＋6.8％と高い伸びを示す中にあっても、7、
8 月平均の新設住宅着工戸数（国土交通省による季節調整値）は、分譲住宅の増加に支えられ
-1.0

### Page 3

3 / 7

4-6月平均比＋1.0％となった（巻末の関連指標②上段右）。しかし、進捗ベースで計上される
GDP統計上は、建設資材等の価格上昇の影響により実質では減少したとみられる。 
設備投資は前期比＋2.0％と予想する。建設投資は減少したものの、機械投資と知的財産生
産物投資（ソフトウェア、研究開発など）が増加したようだ。研究開発投資の増加には、4-6
月期に特殊要因で研究開発サービスの受取（設備投資の控除項目）が大幅に増加したことの反
動も含まれる。経済産業省によると、資本財総供給指数は、国産品と輸入品が共に増加し、7-
9 月期は前期比＋4.9％となった（巻末の関連指標②中段左）。一方、建設投資は、国土交通省
によると、民間非居住用建築・土木の出来高（名目額、大和総研による季節調整値）の7、8月
平均は 4-6月平均比＋1.2％となったが、7月の建設工事費デフレーター（非住宅総合、大和総
研による季節調整値）は前月比＋7.3％と大きく上昇しており、実質では減少したとみられる。 
民間在庫変動は前期比寄与度▲0.0％ptと予想する。仮置き値の原材料在庫は同＋0.1％pt、
仕掛品在庫は同▲0.1％pt となった。その他では製品在庫が同＋ 0.1％pt、流通在庫が同▲
0.1％ptだったとみられる。

公需：公共投資は減少、政府消費は増加と予想 
公共投資は前期比▲1.0％と予想する。国土交通省によると、7、8 月平均の公共工事出来高
（名目額、大和総研による季節調整値）は 4-6月平均比▲2.2％と減少傾向にある（巻末の関連
指標②中段右）。25年度補正予算に盛り込まれた公共事業の執行が一服したとみられる。加え
て、工事費の上昇も重しとなっている。 
政府消費は前期比＋0.2％と予想する。退職金支払いの増加や私立高校授業料の実質無償化、
公立小学校の給食費無償化もあって高い伸びとなった 4-6月期に続き、医療費の増加により 7-
9月期の政府消費も増加したとみられる。 
公的在庫変動は前期比寄与度＋0.3％ptと予想する。4-6月期と異なり、7-9月期には大規模
な石油の国家備蓄放出は行われず、公的在庫変動は実質GDP成長率を押し上げたとみられる。

外需：輸入増が輸出増を上回り、外需寄与度は▲0.2％ptを予想 
輸出は前期比＋0.5％と予想する。サービスは減少したものの、財は増加したと見込んでい
る。財では半導体等製造装置や化学製品、鉱物性燃料などが増加したようだ。サービスでは、
4-6月期に大幅に増加していた研究開発サービスの反動減に加え、産業財産権等使用料やイン
バウンドなども減少したとみている。 
輸入は前期比＋1.8％と予想する。輸出と同様、サービスは減少したものの、財は増加した
と見込んでいる。財では、代替調達の進展により鉱物性燃料が大きく増加したほか、電算機類
も増加したようだ。サービスではアウトバウンドは増加したものの、研究開発サービスや著作
権等使用料などが減少したとみられる。

### Page 4

1

4 / 7

輸入増が輸出増を上回り、純輸出（外需）の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度は前期比▲0.2％
ptになったと予想する。

2．10-12月期の実質 GDPはおおむね横ばいを予想 
2026年 10-12月期の実質 GDP成長率は前期比でおおむね横ばいになると見込んでいる。個人
消費は小幅に増加、設備投資は増加すると予想する。輸出はAI関連需要を背景とした米国向け
輸出を中心に増加するものの、輸入の伸びが上回り、外需はマイナスになるとみている。 
個人消費は小幅に増加すると予想する。厚生労働省「毎月勤労統計調査」の現金給与総額は
前年同月比で 3％を超える伸びが続いていることに加え、10月以降は各地で最低賃金が引き上
げられることから、名目賃金の高い伸びは続くだろう。一方、9月の消費者物価指数（除く生
鮮食品）は前年同月比＋2.1％と、8月から 0.4％pt伸び幅が拡大した。政策要因による影響を
差し引いたとしても、食料品等を中心に物価上昇率は高まっており、実質賃金上昇率を押し下
げるだろう。エネルギー価格についても、電気・ガス料金補助は12月使用分（1月請求分）か
ら再開されるものの、現行の補助は 9月使用分（10月請求分）で一旦終了するため、10-12月
期の物価は押し上げられる。加えて、ガソリン補助金の基準価格が12月以降引き上げられるこ
とや、燃料費・原料費調整制度によって資源価格高騰の影響がラグを持って電気・ガス料金に
反映されることも物価の押し上げ要因となろう1。 
設備投資は増加すると予想する。日本銀行「全国企業短期経済観測調査」 （日銀短観）によ
ると、9月調査時点での 2026年度の設備投資計画（全規模全産業、除く土地、含むソフトウェ
ア・研究開発）は前年度比＋9.3％と、前年の 9月調査時点（同＋9.5％）と同程度の伸びが示
された。建設資材価格と労務費の上昇が建設投資を下押しするものの、省力化投資やAI関連投
資といった設備投資の増加基調は続くと見込む。 
住宅投資は建設資材と労務費上昇の影響を受けて減少すると見込んでいる。公共投資は工事
費の高騰を背景に横ばい圏で推移すると予想する。政府消費は高齢化などに伴う医療費・介護
費増により増加すると見込んでいる。 
輸出は増加すると予想する。米国の設備投資は中東情勢をめぐる不透明感や金利上昇の中に
あっても、AI関連需要を背景に増加が続くだろう。米国内での設備投資の増加は米国向け輸出
の追い風となり、財輸出は増加すると見込んでいる。一方、インバウンドは中国政府による日
本への渡航自粛要請が続く中、小幅な増加にとどまるだろう。 
輸入は増加すると予想する。経済産業省によると、9月の原油調達について、バブ・エル・
マンデブ海峡経由で調達予定であった原油の輸送ルートをスエズ運河経由に切り替えたことで

1 12月以降の電気・ガス料金補助とガソリン補助金に関する政府の対応については、首相官邸「中東情勢を踏
まえた『緊急的激変緩和措置』の今後の対応についての会見」（2026年10月 29日）を参照。

### Page 5

1

5 / 7

到着が遅れた分は備蓄原油の既放出分で対応したという2。10月は前年平月比 10割の調達が実
現できる見通しとのことであり、11月以降も同量を確保できるのであれば、財輸入は押し上げ
られるだろう。 
リスク要因は中東情勢の悪化とAI関連需要の下振れである。中東情勢が悪化し、原油価格が
さらに高騰すれば、物価高を通じて個人消費を一段と下押しするだろう。また、足元の米国経
済では設備投資が景気を下支えしており、とりわけデータセンターや半導体などのIT関連投資
がけん引役となっている。10月半ばにかけて、一部AI関連企業の財務上の脆弱性が意識され、
株価が下落する局面があったが、こうした懸念がAI関連企業全体に広がれば、外部資金への依
存度が高い企業を中心に設備投資が鈍化し得る3。その場合、米国向け輸出が下押しされる可能
性があり、注意が必要だ。

2 詳細は、第 5回中東情勢に伴う重要物資の安定的な供給確保のためのタスクフォース（2026年 10月2日）
における経済産業省提出資料を参照。 
3 詳細は、矢作大祐・藤原翼「米国経済見通し CoreWeave問題をどうみるか」（大和総研レポート、2026年
10月22日）を参照。

2026年 10月 30日 全7頁

Indicators Update 
2026年 9月鉱工業生産 
自動車工業が挽回生産で持ち直し、生産全体は3カ月ぶりに上昇

経済調査部 エコノミスト ビリング 安奈

[要約] 
◼ 2026年 9月の生産指数は前月比＋2.2％と、コンセンサス（同＋1.2％、Bloomberg調
査）を上回り、3 カ月ぶりに上昇した。自動車工業は挽回生産で持ち直したほか、AI
関連需要で生産用機械工業なども増産し、生産全体を押し上げた。経済産業省は基調
判断を「一進一退」に据え置いた。 
◼ 先行きの生産指数は緩やかに上昇する見込み。AI関連需要などが引き続き国内生産を
押し上げよう。ただし、金利高による海外需要の下振れなどが生産を下押しするリス
クには注意が必要だ。 
◼ 2026年 11月 9日に公表予定の 9月分の景気動向指数は、先行 CIが前月差▲0.9ptの
117.1、一致CIが同＋0.7ptの 119.5と予想する。この予測値に基づくと、9月の基調
判断は機械的に「改善」に据え置かれる。

### Page 2

【生産】自動車工業は挽回生産で持ち直し、AI関連需要で生産用機械工業も増産 
2026年 9月の生産指数は前月比＋2.2％と、コンセンサス（同＋1.2％、Bloomberg調査）を
上回り、3カ月ぶりに上昇した。経済産業省は基調判断を「一進一退」に据え置いた。 
生産指数を業種別に見ると、15業種中 8業種が前月から上昇した。自動車工業（前月比＋
5.5％）は 2カ月ぶりに上昇した。品目別では、普通乗用車（同＋3.3％）や駆動伝導・操縦装
置部品（同＋7.0％）などが増産となった。前月は熊本地震や台風による工場稼働停止や部品
供給の停滞を受けて生産が落ち込んだが、9 月は生産体制がおおむね正常化したことで挽回生
産が進み、自動車工業の生産は持ち直した。また、生産用機械工業（同＋3.4％）は4カ月連続
で上昇した。品目別では、AI関連製品である半導体製造装置（同＋13.8％）のほか、化学機械
（同＋34.9％）などが増産となった。経済産業省「鉱工業出荷内訳表」によると、生産用機械
工業は国内向け（同＋7.8％）が好調だった。汎用・業務用機械工業（同＋4.8％）では、コン
ベヤ（同＋142.0％）や運搬用クレーン（同＋35.5％）が増産となり、2カ月ぶりの上昇となっ
た。一方、鉄鋼・非鉄金属工業（前月比▲1.6％）や窯業・土石製品工業（同▲1.9％）など 7
業種は前月から低下した。鉄鋼・非鉄金属工業では亜鉛めっき鋼板（同▲10.0％）などが、窯
業・土石製品工業ではガラス繊維（短繊維）（同▲6.3％）などが減産となった。 
財別では、生産財（前月比＋1.0％）、耐久消費財（同＋5.3％）、非耐久消費財（同＋0.9％）、
建設財（同＋2.5％）、資本財（除．輸送機械） （同＋1.0％）のいずれも増産となった。設備投
資の関連指標である資本財（除．輸送機械）はAI需要を背景に大幅な増産が見込まれる。

【出荷・在庫】出荷指数は自動車工業などを中心に2カ月ぶりに上昇 
2026年 9月の出荷指数は前月比＋0.6％と 2カ月ぶりに上昇した。業種別では、自動車工業
（同＋2.3％）や汎用・業務用機械工業（同＋5.5％）など 15業種中 8業種が上昇した。財別に
見ると、非耐久消費財と資本財（除．輸送機械）、建設財は低下した一方、耐久消費財と生産
財は上昇した。在庫指数は同＋2.5％、在庫率指数は同＋2.0％だった。

生産予測調査による。

【先行き】生産指数は緩やかに上昇する見込み 
先行きの生産指数は、緩やかな上昇基調を辿るとみている。AI関連需要などが引き続き国内
生産の押し上げ要因となる。ただし、世界的な金利高、特に米国の金利高による海外需要の下
振れなどが生産を下押しするリスクには注意が必要だ。 
AI関連財の拡大は 2024年から日本国内の生産を押し上げてきており、今後も生産の押し上
げ要因になると見込まれる1。26年 8月の世界半導体販売額（3カ月平均）を見ると、前年比＋
144.3％と強い伸びが続いている。米国の販売額（同＋180.9％）がけん引する中、日本（同＋
62.3％）2も力強さを示している。また、日本半導体製造装置協会によると、26年9月の半導体
製造装置販売高（3カ月平均）は同＋48.2％と高い伸びとなった3。AI関連を中心とする旺盛な
半導体需要が反映されているとみられる。ただし、米国の金利高でAI関連需要が下振れする可
能性には注意が必要だ。高金利下で米企業の資金調達負担が増え、多額の資金を必要とする AI
関連投資が鈍化すれば4、日本の生産も輸出減を通して下押しされるリスクがある。 
なお、中東情勢による悪影響は引き続き注視する必要がある。米国の中間選挙後に対イラン
攻撃が再開される可能性も報じられており5、原油供給をめぐる不安は引き続き残る。一方、今
年 2月末に始まった中東危機を受け、ホルムズ海峡の輸送停滞への対応として政府が国家備蓄
原油を放出して以降、初めて買い戻しが実施された6。代替調達が進み、国家備蓄原油の取り崩
しが一服し、備蓄の補充が始まったことは好材料だ。ただし、情勢が再び悪化すれば、原材料
不足を通じて生産が下押しされる可能性がある。 
製造工業生産予測調査では、10月の生産指数は前月比＋5.1％と見込まれる。業種別では 11
業種中 9業種が上昇する見通しだ。電子部品・デバイス工業（同＋16.6％）で集積回路などの
増産が計画されているほか、生産用機械工業（同＋15.5％）では半導体・フラットパネルディ
スプレイ製造装置など、AI関連製品の増産が全体を押し上げる見通しだ。生産指数全体の計画
のバイアスを補正した試算値（最頻値）7で見ると、10月は同＋3.9％と上昇が見込まれる。 
11月の生産指数は前月比＋0.8％と見込まれている。業種別では 11業種中 7業種が上昇する
見通しだ。輸送機械工業（同＋4.3％）では乗用車など、汎用・業務用機械工業（同＋4.8％）
では計測・分析機器などの増産が見込まれている。

1 詳細は、神田慶司・秋元虹輝・菊池慈陽・横田凱「日本経済見通し：2026年6月」（大和総研レポート、
2026年6月23日）を参照。 
2 World Semiconductor Trade Statistics “Historical Billings Report”（最終閲覧日：2026年10月 30
日） 
3 日本半導体製造装置協会「2026年9月度販売高（SEAJ速報値）日本製半導体製造装置（3ヶ月平均）」
（2026年10月 23日） 
4 詳細は、矢作大祐・藤原翼「米国経済見通し CoreWeave問題をどうみるか」（大和総研レポート、2026年
10月22日）を参照。 
5 「トランプ政権が湾岸諸国に対イラン攻撃への準備を要請 米中間選挙後に最終判断か」（産経新聞、2026
年 10月 29日）

28日） 
7 生産計画は生産実績よりも上振れした値となることが多いため、生産指数全体の計画のバイアスを補正した
試算値（最頻値）が公表されている。

【26年9月景気動向指数】先行CIは低下、一致CIは上昇を見込む 
鉱工業指数の結果を受けて、2026年 11月 9日に公表予定の 9月分の景気動向指数は、先行
CIが前月差▲0.9ptの 117.1、一致 CIが同＋0.7ptの 119.5と予想する（図表 4） 。先行CIで
は構成指標のうち、鉱工業用生産財在庫率指数（逆サイクル）や最終需要財在庫率指数（逆サ
イクル）、東証株価指数などが前月から悪化した。一致 CIでは構成指標のうち、生産指数（鉱
工業）や鉱工業用生産財出荷指数、有効求人倍率（除学卒）などが改善した。この予測値に基
づくと、2026年9月の基調判断は機械的に「改善」に据え置かれる。

図表 4：景気動向指数（先行 CI、一致 CI）と基調判断の推移

（出所）内閣府統計より大和総研作成

- 1 - 
個股報告

毅嘉(2402) 
光通產品高速增長，海外產能持續擴張 
投資評等 目標價 
逢低買進 $71 
前次投資建議：無

報告日期：2026.10.27 研 究 員：邱岳霖

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 26Q4 光通訊量產，惟缺料與淡季影響營運動能，EPS 0.41元。 
➢ AI 光通訊放量帶動營收倍增，mSAP量產為毛利率改善關鍵，2027 年 EPS 3.1 元，考量公
司切入高速成長之光通賽道，未來幾年營運將高速增長，兆豐投顧給予目標價 71 元(23X 
27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 23.9%，投資評等為「逢低買進」 。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/10/22 收盤價： 57.30 ➢ 光通訊散熱型金屬外殼已於26Q3量產 ，FlipChip打件與
液冷測漏板於26Q4導入量產，預期光通訊營收佔比由
1%升至6%，然MLCC、DSP、PCB三項缺料影響，同
時進入消費性產品淡季，預估毅嘉 26Q4營收32.7億
元，QoQ -4.3%，毛利率14.5%，季增0.2ppt，稅後淨
利1.3億元，QoQ +23.9%，EPS 0.41元。 
➢ 展望後市，AI光通訊將成為毅嘉主要成長動能，預期AI
相關營收佔比將由26Q4之6%躍升至2027年40%， 係因
800G光收發模組需求持續強勁，目前主要客戶明年總
需求為27-30KK，此外1.6T/3.2T世代將於2028至2029
年接續拉升，帶動Flip Chip打件需求大幅擴大，而毅嘉
持續在馬來西亞擴產，雖帶動營收顯著提升，不過因採
turnkey出貨，料件佔BOM cost約80%至90%，故毛利
率僅約5%至8%，將稀釋整體毛利率。後續改善關鍵在
於mSAP高密度電路板，目前已啟動客戶樣品打樣，預
期27Q3量產，毛利率可達40%以上；此外散熱事業營
收倍增並轉虧為盈，攜手杰華精密切入光模組散熱外
殼，加上IMS延伸至儲能與機器人應用，將使產品組合
逐步轉佳。綜上所述，兆豐投顧預估毅嘉 2027年營收
248.9億元，YoY +94.9%，毛利率11.5%，年減2.5ppt，
稅後淨利9.5億元，YoY +159%，EPS 3.1元。考量公
司切入高速成長之光通賽道 ，未來幾年營運將高速增
長，兆豐投顧給予目標價71元(23X 27(F)PER) ，潛在
上漲空間23.9%，投資評等為「逢低買進」 。

- 2 - 
個股報告 
資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析

毅嘉科技為軟板及機構整合廠  
毅嘉科技成立於 1983 年，初期以生產手機按鍵為主，於 2000 年掛牌上市
並跨足軟板製造領域，2020 年延伸至硬板打件並在跨入車用模組，2024 年
將戰略重心擴張至光通訊領域，從早期供應光收發模組用軟硬結合板，2026
年切入光收發模組 Flip Chip SMT，於 26Q3 完成認證並於 26Q4 放量生產，
預計 2027 年再導入 mSAP 高密度電路板，預期 27Q3 開始量產，此外毅嘉
於 26Q2 投資光通訊零組件廠商杰華精密(持股 46.25%)跨入光模組金屬外
殼。

毅嘉現主營軟板及機構整合業務：26Q3 應用別營收組成為車用 62%(智能座
艙 40%，連接板 19%，內飾件 3%)，其他消費 10%，遊戲 9%，穿戴設備
8%，其他產品 7%，手機 3%，光通訊 1%(26Q4 預估升至 6%)。生產基地
位於台灣林口廠(研發中心、散熱模組、IMS 研發中心)、大陸蘇州廠(車用軟
板、空板、 SMT、Mini LED)、大陸中山廠(穿戴裝置)、馬來西亞檳城及吉
打廠(25Q3 完工，產品為機構件、光通訊、車用、儲能 )，越南及匈牙利設
有銷售據點，北美據點評估中(德州)。

圖一、應用別營收組成

- 3 - 
個股報告 
26Q3 營收創今年新高，然新廠成本與匯損壓抑獲利，EPS 0.33元 
毅嘉(2402)公告 26Q3財報：營收 34.2 億元，QoQ +4.4%/YoY +18.4%，再
創近年新高，主要受惠車用及消費性電子產品需求成長帶動，毛利率 14.3%，
季增 0.1ppt，然因馬來西亞新廠初期營運成本與費用增加，營業利益較上季
衰退 10.5%，同時受人民幣升值產生匯兌損失，故稅後淨利 1 億元，QoQ 
-16.0%/YoY -45.1%，EPS 0.33元。

26Q4 光通訊量產，惟缺料與淡季影響營運動能，EPS 0.41元 
光通訊散熱型金屬外殼已於 26Q3 量產，FlipChip打件與液冷測漏板於 26Q4
導入量產，預期光通訊營收佔比由 1%升至 6%，然 MLCC、DSP、PCB 三
項缺料影響，同時進入消費性產品淡季，兆豐投顧預估毅嘉 26Q4 營收 32.7
億元，QoQ -4.3%/YoY +20.0%，毛利率 14.5%，季增 0.2ppt，年減 5.3ppt，
稅後淨利 1.3 億元，QoQ +23.9%/YoY -32.9%，EPS 0.41元。

AI 光通訊放量帶動營收倍增，mSAP量產為毛利率改善關鍵，2027年 EPS 
3.1元 
展望後市，AI 光通訊將成為毅嘉主要成長動能，預期 AI 相關營收佔比將由
26Q4之 6%躍升至 2027 年 40%，係因 800G 光收發模組需求持續強勁，目
前主要客戶明年總需求為 27-30KK，此外 1.6T/3.2T 世代將於 2028 至 2029
年接續拉升，帶動 Flip Chip 打件需求大幅擴大，而毅嘉持續在馬來西亞擴
產，Flip Chip 產線將由今年 3條提升至 2027 年 8-10條，2028 年進一步擴
充至 24條，雖帶動營收顯著提升，不過因採 turnkey 出貨，料件佔 BOM cost
約 80%至 90%，故毛利率僅約 5%至 8%，將稀釋整體毛利率。後續改善關
鍵在於 mSAP高密度電路板，目前已啟動客戶樣品打樣，預期 27Q3量產，
毛利率可達 40%以上；此外散熱事業營收倍增並轉虧為盈，攜手杰華精密切
入光模組散熱外殼，加上IMS 延伸至儲能與機器人應用，將使產品組合逐步
轉佳。綜上所述，兆豐投顧預估毅嘉2027 年營收 248.9 億元，YoY +94.9%，
毛利率 11.5%，年減2.5ppt，稅後淨利9.5 億元，YoY +159%，EPS 3.1 元。

### Page 1

M
Idea
Chroma Ate Inc.  | Asia Pacific
2H26 Driven by Semis and
Photonics; 2027 Seen as
Another Strong Year
A unique combination of power, electrical and optical testing
tool capabilities position the company well in AI deployment.
Stay OW.
We see 3Q26 EPS of NT$13.71: Chroma's 3Q26 revenue came in at NT$17,926mn,
up 32% QoQ and 180% YoY. We believe the strength was driven mainly by the
semiconductor and photonics businesses, while the power testing business likely

remained at an elevated level. Within the semiconductor and photonics businesses,
SL T  should benefit from AI HPC customers' ramping; meanwhile, optical testing
tools for transceivers and, to a lesser extent, CPO, are also seen as growth drivers
during the quarter. We estimate GM of 60.5% (flat QoQ) as scale tailwinds should
be largely offset by a less favorable product mix, while OPM should expand by
0.5ppt QoQ to 39.3% on some operating leverage. Overall, we expect EPS of NT

$13.71 (+13% QoQ/+15% YoY).
Growth momentum seen sustained into 2027: Looking ahead, we expect Chroma
to see another year of strong growth, supported by the power testing (51% growth
for 42% mix) and semi & photonics businesses (58% growth for 40% mix).
• Power testing: The ever-increasing power consumption of AI clusters should
continue to drive demand for higher spec testing tools. Along with our
forecast of a 75% CAGR for global power supply unit demand in the next

three years (see report), we think this should stay a major driver for Chroma.
• SLT: We are seeing more HPC chip designers adopting the system level
testing process to make sure defects won't be incorporated into the AI
systems. As the first mover in this market. Chroma should benefit from
increasing chip volume as well as higher TDP, in our view.
• Photonics: Transceivers should continue to be the mainstream solution;
architectures such as CPO and NPO also need testing tools.

• Emerging business: We expect Chroma, by leveraging its existing customer
relationship and technology expertise, to gain more traction in FT handlers
and burn-in ovens for AI HPCs in 2027. These are the markets that Chroma
previously focused less on, so they represent new opportunities.
Stay OW: We remain constructive on Chroma for its testing equipment's unique
combination of power, electrical and optical capabilities, allowing it to capture
growth opportunities in various aspects of AI deployment. Our PT remains NT

$2,800 as our estimate increases are offset by a lower target multiple of 40x 2027e
P/E (previously 45x) to factor in generally more conservative sentiment toward the
AI space now. This implies a PEG of 0.6 (vs. 1.0 over the past decade).

A Closer Look
Test instrument and ATS segment set to reach another
record high in 2026
Revenue for the segment increased 55% YoY to NT$10,546mn (37% of the mix) in 2025.
We estimate it will rise 118% YoY to NT$24,564mn (40% of the mix) in 2026. The ever-
increasing power consumption of AI clusters should continue to drive demand for higher
spec testing tools. Along with our forecast of a 75% CAGR for global power supply unit

demand in the coming three years (see our report), this should remain a major driver for
Chroma, in our view.

Multiple drivers in the semiconductor business
System-level testing  (SL T) business
SL T should benefit from the capex cycle of NVIDIA's Rubin generation GPU, AMD's MI450
GPU and more ASIC accelerators.  Management pointed out that discrete testing tools are
still preferred  by major customers.  We are also seeing more HPC chip designers adopting

the system level testing process to make sure defects won't be incorporated into the AI
systems. We believe Chroma, as the first mover in this market, should benefit from
increasing chip volume, as well as higher TDP.
Metrology business
The metrology business should continue to benefit from current advanced packaging
architectures from its foundry and OSAT customers. It is confident that the performance
of its tools can win future architecture projects, which our industry checks suggest should

have higher metrology density.

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
Exhibit 3: Chroma –  Offerings for Metrology in Advanced Packaging
Source: Chroma
Chroma's metrology equipment employs white light interferometry technology. The basic
working principle is that white light is emitted on the reference mirror and the sample,
which are placed at the same distance from the light source. The reflected beams from
the reference mirror and sample surface are then received by a light sensor.
Interference occurs when two light beams collide, which could cause  them to either

strengthen or weaken, depending on the phase of the two light beams. Thus, by
measuring the intensity of the combined light beams, the distance between light sensor
and multiple points on the target surface can be calculated through an algorithm. This can
then be used to measure the dimensions of the structure or the alignment of layers.
Exhibit 4: Concept of Light Interference
Source: Keyence
Exhibit 5: Setup of Interferometer
Source: Wikipedia
CPO contribution to start from 2026

Chroma has been qualified for optical engine testing in Insertion 3 and system-level
testing in Insertion 4, and there might be incremental opportunities if supply chain players'
progress falls behind for testing equipment in Insertion 2. Over the longer term, Chroma
also  aspires to participate in the PIC optical/electrical/reliability testing in Insertion 1.

FT handlers to add another revenue stream
In HPC testing, Chroma used to focus mostly on the SL T side, although it did unveil its first
HPC FT handler earlier this year and is now going through the qualification process. We
believe this is a natural extension of its expertise and customer relationships accumulated
in SL Ts. We expect Chroma to gain more traction in FT handlers for AI HPCs in 2027.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Our price target (base case scenario value) remains  NT$2,800: Our PT is based on a
target multiple of 40x 2027e P/E (vs. 45x 2027e previously).   We lowered our multiple to
factor in recent volatility in market sentiment toward AI.   We view our multiple as justified
based on our estimated 68% operating profit CAGR over 2025-28. This implies a PEG of
0.6 vs. its average of 1.0 in the past decade, when the stock was trading at an average of

~19x forward P/E with an operating profit CAGR of 20%.
Bull case scenario value – NT$3,900 (unchanged): We apply a 55x multiple (vs. 62x P/E
previously)  to our earnings estimate for 2027.  This scenario reflects stronger  5G and HPC
demand, driving more earnings upside.
Bear case scenario value – NT$1,100 (unchanged): We apply a 16x P/E multiple (vs. 18x
P/E previously) to our earnings estimate for 2027. This scenario factors in weaker HPC
revenue as hyperscalers start to focus more on ROI for AI capex than they did in the past

couple of years.
Risks:
• Softer-than-expected AI capex
• Slower progress in advanced packaging capacity expansion
• Weaker consumer electronics and automobile related demand

Risk Reward - Chroma Ate Inc. (2360.TW)
NT$2,800.00
Base case, 40x 2027e P/E. We use P/E as our valuation methodology because we think it
better captures Chroma's earnings growth momentum. The stock traded at an average
valuation of ~19x P/E during 2014-25 with an operating proﬁt CAGR of 20%, implying a PEG
of 1.0. We think our target multiple of 40x is justiﬁed by the 68% operating proﬁt CAGR we
project for 2025-28.

MS Rating
▪ Multiple drivers for semi business growth:
SLT will see the ﬁrst year of capex cycle for
the Rubin generation GPU, along with
adoption at MI450 and ASIC accelerators.
The metrology business should grow along
with advanced packaging capacity
expansion. On CPO, Chroma was qualiﬁed
for optical engine testing in Insertion 3 and

system-level testing in Insertion 4.
▪ Power testing business continues to ride
the AI capex cycle: Migration into the
±400/800VDC power rack architecture
should be a positive development, while
infrastructure builds in China should drive
better ESS demand.
▪ Stay OW: Our PT of NT$2,800 is based on
target P/E of 40x 2027e vs. an average of
~19x forward P/E since 2010.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Update
Hon Precision  | Asia Pacific
CPO  handler on track for 1Q27
mass production; stay OW
Key takeaways from yesterday's call:
• CPO/CPC: Has shipped  a small volume of  Insertion 4 OE CPO FT
qualification handlers in 4Q26, which are on track for mass production in
1Q27. Management expects qualification to start in November and end before
the year-end. It's also working on a CPC handler project with Japanese ATE
and a US customer who showed strong interest.

• Mega Tray Handler: Around 20-30% of customer bookings are Mega Trays,
while full system ASP would increase by at least 30% from original handlers.
Has also shipped the handler for customer qualification.
• Preliminary 2027 outlook:  To see strong revenue growth  in 2H27 from
Mega Trays and CPO-related demand as these higher ASP solutions will start
mass production in 1H27, with revenue recognition typically taking 3-6
months.
• Capacity outlook: 2027 remains on a 50% Y/Y pace, while the equipment

unit growth might be lower than 50% given the higher complexity of new
equipment.
Reiterate OW: Hon Precision is a key beneficiary of the expanding AI testing TAM.
We see the recent pullback as an attractive entry point, with the stock now trading
at 24x 2027e EPS vs. a 2025–28e earnings CAGR of >70%.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Hon Precision (7769.TW)
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture
the long-term value. We assume a 9.8% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium
6.0%), a payout ratio of 65%, a medium-term growth rate of 15%, and a terminal growth rate
of 4.0%. Our price target implies 77x 2026e, 45x 2027e and 27x 2028e P/Es.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected AI demand

n Stronger-than-expected market share expansion
n Faster-than-expected CPO development with more testing needs emerging
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected AI demand
n Stronger-than-expected market share gain from peers
n Slower-than-expected CPO development

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

Idea
3Q26 earnings missed: Please see Exhibit 1 .
4Q guidance reflects weak NB demand: Parade is guiding rev. to decline 24% Q/Q
at mid-point, 32% below consensus. We overestimated the stability of demand from
standard-plus customers, while demand from standard customers remains very
weak, as expected. Based on the current demand outlook into 1Q27, it is difficult to
see a V-shaped recovery. That said, we appreciate Parade's supply chain
management and its ability to pass cost pressures on to customers. We see Parade's

TTED business offering L T opportunities amid PCB price hikes.
New business ramp could take time: We previously modelled the company’s ASIC-
like business contributing 15-20% of revenue in 2027-28. Given the complexity of
the chip design and weak DT demand, we believe we were too optimistic. As for the
TTED business, the company expects shipments to exceed 5mn units in 2026, which
could account for ~8% of 2026 revenue. The company expects NB shipments to

remain similar in 2027 vs. 2026, subject to DRAM pricing. Currently, we do not
expect DRAM pricing to decline meaningfully.
Double downgrade to UW; PT NT$444: We cut our earnings forecasts mainly to
reflect weaker 4Q guidance and a lower contribution from the ASIC-like business.
We lowered 2026/27/28e earnings by 14%/39%/43%. The company is now trading at
17x 2027e EPS. We like Parade's long-term growth potential but would need to see a
meaningful resumption in business growth to turn more positive. (continued on the

next page)

 Idea
2

3Q26 earnings review: 3Q revenue was NT$4.2bn, -2% Q/Q, -4% Y/Y, and 4% above MSe; 
3Q GM was 40.3%, flattish Q/Q, -2.2ppt Y/Y and 2.3ppt below MSe; 3Q EPS was NT$7.67, 
flat Q/Q, -27% Y/Y and 6% below MSe. Weaker 2H26 demand has been due to OEM pull-
ins in 1H, compressed consumer demand due to price hikes, and compressed enterprise 
budgets due to allocation to AI spending.
Exhibit 1: 3Q26 earnings review
Source: Company data, Morgan Stanley Research (E) estimates
Why it's not too late to downgrade now: The weak 4Q26 outlook lowers the earnings 
base, while visibility on a sustained recovery remains limited. With NB shipments expected 
to remain broadly flat in 2027, TTED growth and initial ASIC-like contributions may be 
insufficient to offset weakness in the core business. The risk of prolonged earnings 
pressure and a slower-than-expected ramp in new businesses supports our more cautious 
view beyond the near-term reset.

business.

Parade: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target from  NT$818 to NT$444:  Our price target is our base case
scenario value, which we continue to derive from a residual income model. Our bull and
bear case scenario values are cut from NT$948 to NT$755, and from NT$418 to NT$288,
respectively.
Intermediate growth cut to 9.4% from 11.4% to reflect slower ramp up of new businesses.
Other key RI assumptions are unchanged:
• 9.8% cost of equity (beta 1.3, risk-free rate 2% and risk premium 6%)

• Payout ratio of 50%
• Terminal growth rate of 4.0%

Source: Morgan Stanley Research (E) estimates

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 1

EQUITY: FABLESS
MediaTek   2454.TW 2454 TT
Global Markets Research
30 October 2026
Dispels recent market noises with upside surprise; retain Buy
Quick Note
Maintain Buy and TP of TWD5,800
We recently noted that MediaTek’s earnings call could prove to be a positive catalyst for 
its share price, considering a number of unfounded concerns and noises had taken center 
stage before the call (Preview: market concerns overdone ). However, we believe some of 
MTK’s messages during earnings call turned out to be even more bullish than we had 
expected, including: (1) TPU v.8 contribution of USD2.5bn in 4Q26E (vs. previous 
guidance of more than USD2bn , and Street concerns around MTK potentially missing the 
USD2bn target); (2) management now expects ASIC sales in 2027E to reach the high end 
(USD16bn) of its prior guidance range (USD14-16bn) vs. our forecast of USD14bn; (3) 
TPU v.9 schedule is on track for 1Q28E mass production (a powerful clarification vs. 
Street expectation of 1-2 quarters’ delay); (4) management is very confident about v.9 
EMIB-T future yield trend after resolving 2-3 key issues (implying no CoWoS back-up plan, 
vs. Street expectation of a CoWoS-based v.9 project being opened); (5) Mediatek 
announced that it has secured a 2nd ASIC customer with sales contribution from 2028E; 
(6) ASIC-driven operating leverage will kick in from 4Q26E (guidance suggesting 19% 
OPM in 4Q26E, vs our estimate of 18.6% and 3Q26’s 16%; and management expects 
2027E OPM to be above 20%, vs our estimate of 22%). The only downside surprise might 
be its 4Q26E sales guidance, which suggests 25%-32% q-q growth vs. our estimate of 
34% q-q. We would watch if this is a low bar for MediaTek to beat. GPM guidance of 
45%+-1.5% is 1pp lower than previous quarter’s range (46%+-1.5%; and 46.3% actual in 
3Q26) due to ASIC — in line with our forecast of 45.4%. Overall, we expect the share 
price to react positively to the earnings call and, thus, reiterate our Buy rating and 
TWD5,800 TP, based on 25x unchanged 2027-28 EPS estimate. The stock is currently 
trading at 16.9x.

MediaTek: 3Q26 analysts briefing summary
Below comments are from MediaTek’s management, unless otherwise stated :
Results summary
3Q26 revenue was TWD168.1bn (+10.4% q-q, +18.3% y-y), above the high end of
the TWD152.2–159.8bn guidance.
The beat came mainly from better-than-expected customer demand and
improving supply.
○
Growth was driven by volume production of several new Smart Edge projects and
higher DRAM content in TV SoC.
○
•
GM was 46.3% (+0.1pp q-q, -0.2pp y-y), within the 46% ±1.5pp guidance.•

Opex ratio was 30.2% (vs 31.2% in 2Q26 and 30.9% in 3Q25), within the 31% ±2pp
guidance.
•
OPM was 16.1% (+1.1pp q-q, +0.5pp y-y).•
Net income was TWD28.2bn (+14.6% q-q, +10.8% y-y). Net margin was 16.8%
(+0.6pp q-q, -1.1pp y-y).
•
EPS was TWD17.50 (TWD15.28 in 2Q26, TWD15.84 in 3Q25). Non-TIFRS EPS was
TWD17.92.
•
Average FX was TWD31.99/USD (2Q26: 31.60; guidance assumption: 32).•
Prepared remarks
MediaTek completed a USD3.9bn overseas convertible bond offering in 3Q, with

participation from nVidia (NVDA US, Not rated) and Alphabet (GOOGL US, Not
rated).
This strengthens partnerships with key AI infrastructure players and helps secure
capacity for critical components.
○
•
MediaTek continues to make solid progress in both data center and edge devices.
Datacenter ASIC
Current gen: Demand remains very strong. Initial shipments began in
September, and MediaTek is confident of delivering USD2.5bn of revenue in
4Q26.
The 4Q upside comes from robust customer demand. MediaTek has

worked closely with supply chain partners to secure supply, including
substrates.
■
For 2027E, the program is expected to reach the high end of the revenue
target range, about USD16bn, or around 20% of the AI accelerator ASIC
market. This is underpinned by a solid supply outlook, and MediaTek is
working to secure additional capacity to meet demand.
■
•
Next gen: Development is progressing well and is on track for HVM in 1Q28.
The chip is purpose-built for the customer’s next-generation AI

infrastructure. It delivers multiple times higher performance per watt at a
significantly lower TCO.
■
It is expected to strengthen MediaTek’s market position and contribute
substantially to revenue growth.
■
•
Technology: Solid progress continues in 448G SerDes, 3.5D advanced
packaging, and CPO.
•
Broader customer base: MediaTek is expanding beyond silicon into system
and rack solutions. It connects custom xPUs to the NVLink Fusion ecosystem
and also supports UALink.

•
MediaTek continues to expand engagements with several customers.•
○
Edge
The RTX Spark AI PC (N1X), which integrates an nVidia GPU, became
commercially available in mid-October through major PC OEMs. Next-gen
SoC development is already underway.
•
Dimensity 9500 powers the latest Samsung Galaxy AI tablets. This is the third
consecutive generation of Samsung AI tablets using a MediaTek flagship
SoC.
•
○

Q&A
Any changes in ASIC schedule and milestones?
The 2nd ASIC is on track and on schedule.
MediaTek is aware of market noise about the tape-out schedule. The chip has

multiple dies, and a couple have already taped out.
○
The large, more complex die will also tape out on schedule, though it takes about
three weeks longer than the others.
○
•
2028 will be another year of strong y-y growth based on the demand profile. No
details yet, as the supply chain is still being finalized.
•
EMIB-T confidence
MediaTek is working with the US IDM’s packaging process and has built a strong in-
house manufacturing team led by experienced fab veterans.

•
It is working closely with the IDM and substrate suppliers.•
Management has high confidence that yield will improve quickly once two or three key
problems are resolved.
MediaTek is working with both Taiwanese and Japanese suppliers.○
•
MediaTek is not dependent on a single supplier.
TSMC (2330 TT, Buy) remains its most important partner, and the two work
closely across the entire technology chain.
○
•
Next-next gen, TAM and contribution
MediaTek is working very closely with the customer on the next-next generation.•

The customer is aggressive in pushing performance up and TCO down.•
This will be another challenging chip, and the definition will take time to finalize.•
Management will share more when the time is right.•

### Page 4

New ASIC client
A 2nd customer is confirmed, separate from the lead customer.•
The project has kicked off, with first revenue and mass production expected in 2028.•
The customer is not from China.•
Vendor financing for LLM customers?
MediaTek will consider it if necessary and if it is the right customer.•
Returns versus capex have improved over the past six quarters. Management 
believes capex, whether from CSPs or frontier model companies, will generate 
enough revenue and cash flow. Some support may be needed during the transition 
period before cash generation stabilizes.
•
EMIB-T / HBM5 / technology capability and competition
The entry barrier is the ability to integrate multiple technologies into manufacturable, 
scalable products: advanced packaging, HBM5, 448G SerDes, and low power. It is 
not any single technology.
•
Competitors are strong, and MediaTek does not assume it is far ahead. However, it is 
confident it will win its share of the business.
•
NVLink Fusion benefits and customer interest
It is a business model with strong technical content, and it makes it much easier for 
customers to integrate their own designs.
•
The key advantage is much faster time to compute power.•
MediaTek and nVidia are in discussions with several potential customers. Feedback 
is positive, and MediaTek hopes to announce cases in the near term.
•
448G
448G SerDes has already taped out.•
448G is a must going forward. It is an N6 design with very stringent power 
consumption requirements.
•
It will not be on the critical path, and MediaTek will improve interoperability to serve a 
broader customer base.
•

Definition of Nomura Group's equity research rating system and sectors
The rating system is a relative system, indicating expected performance against a specific benchmark identified for each individual stock, 
subject to limited management discretion. An analyst’s target price is an assessment of the current intrinsic fair value of the stock based on an 
appropriate valuation methodology determined by the analyst. Valuation methodologies include, but are not limited to, discounted cash flow 
analysis, expected return on equity and multiple analysis. Analysts may also indicate expected absolute upside/downside relative to the stated 
target price, defined as (target price - current price)/current price.

SECTORS
A 'Bullish' stance, indicates that the analyst expects the sector to outperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Neutral' stance, 
indicates that the analyst expects the sector to perform in line with the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Bearish' stance, indicates that 
the analyst expects the sector to underperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. Sectors that are labelled as 'Not rated' or shown as 
'N/A' are not assigned ratings. Benchmarks are as follows: United States: S&P 500; Europe: Dow Jones STOXX 600; Global Emerging 
Markets (ex-Asia): MSCI Emerging Markets ex-Asia. Japan/Asia ex-Japan: Sector ratings are not assigned.

Target Price
A Target Price, if discussed, indicates the analyst’s forecast for the share price with a 12-month time horizon, reflecting in part the analyst's 
estimates for the company's earnings. The achievement of any target price may be impeded by general market and macroeconomic trends, and 
by other risks related to the company or the market, and may not occur if the company's earnings differ from estimates.

公司拜訪快報

家登(3680)：EUV POD、中國 FOUP 雙軌成長，展望正向 
建議：維持買進

一、3Q26 營收創高，EUV POD 放量與產品組合優化推
升獲利 
家登 3Q26 營收 27.1 億元 ，+16.9% QoQ，+62.9% YOY，
受惠 EUV POD 及 FOUP 出貨同步成長，營運規模持續
擴大，除台灣主要客戶需求穩健外，美系大客戶出貨量
亦大幅增加，帶動高單價產品營收貢獻，另中國市場
FOUP 需求回溫 ， 多個客戶同步拉貨挹注營收 。 考量EUV 
POD 及中國FOUP 均為影響公司獲利結構的重要產品，
預期在出貨規模擴大、產品組合維持有利的情況下，家
登 3Q26 營收與獲利可望同步展現強勁成長。在產能利
用率提升、加上產品組合優化下，預估毛利率上升至
43.9%， 季增1.2%、 年增4.8%， 營業利益達5.3 億元，
營業利益率 19.7%，季增 2.4%、年增 10.6%，稅後歸
屬母公司淨利 4.2 億元，EPS 4.41 元。 
二、4Q26 營運維持高檔 
展望 4Q26，EUV POD 及 FOUP 需求延續，加上台灣
大客戶先進製程產能擴張，帶動載具需求續，預估家登
4Q26 營收 27.7 億元，+2.3% QoQ，+22.0% YoY。預
期稼動率及生產效率提升將改善毛利率，惟考量海外布
局、折舊攤提、人力擴編等因子下，以保守態度估計毛
利率略提升至 44.2%，季增 0.3 百分點，年增 0.9 百分
點 ， 不排除後續上修空間，預估營益率19.9%，季減 0.2
百分點 ， 年減增2.4 百分點 ， 歸屬母公司淨利4.2 億元，
EPS 為 4.35 元。 
三、先進製程擴產和中國 FOUP 回溫為公司成長雙動能 
家登的光罩載具以 EUV POD 為主要成長引擎，隨 AI
發展帶動先進製程光罩層數、客戶開案量及載具汰換需
求皆顯著增加，預期 EUV POD 用量穩健提升，就產業
端觀察 ， 台灣大客戶先進製程節點已佔其整體營收79%，
且投資金額、規模持續擴大，而 ASML 最新法說也顯示
EUV 設備需求強勁，為 EUV 供應鏈的中長期成長提供
支撐，另外，除台灣主要客戶需求穩健外，今年美系客
戶出貨量亦大幅增加，預期 2027 年延續有機成長。晶
圓載具方面，中國 FOUP 需求明顯回溫，且客戶組成較
過往更加多元，整體貢獻有望回到前波高峰。 
四、先進製程投資延續，2026-2027 年成長可期 
AI 發展推動先進製程進入一波新投資循環，ASML 近期
法說會表示，EUV 設備需求維持高檔，直至 2028 年都
供不應求，已有客戶針對 2029 年後訂單展開討論，而
台灣大客戶亦提及 2030 年將導入 High-NA EUV 進行量
產，我們認為 AI 相關需求不墜，可支持家登 EUV POD
中長期需求，惟實際營收貢獻仍視客戶開案、光罩使用
量及載具採購進度而定。獲利方面，內部效率提升與成
本反映有助改善部分產品利潤，預估 2026/2027 年營收
為 96.5/117.1 億元，+31.4%/21.3% YoY，EPS 
16.83/22.70 元，目前股價依 2026 年 EPS 推估，約位
於 30.5 倍本益比，維持買進評等，目標價615 元(35.0x 
3Q26-2Q27PER)。

研 究  報 告                
上品(4770.TT) 
產業：化學工業 
AI 需求帶動訂單攀升，擴產與高毛利產品推升獲利成⻑  
 投資評價與建議 
考量AI驅動全球半導體擴產 ， 帶動氟素設備 、 化學品槽⾞與建廠⼯程訂單成⻑ ，
且低毛利舊單消化、高毛利產品放量及兩岸產能擴充推升毛利率，參考同業日
本AGC、Daikin PE區間13-21倍，基於2027年EPS給予17倍PER。 
 重點評論分析 
AI需求推升訂單動能 ：3Q26單季營收季增22.5%至16.0億元 ， 受惠AI帶動國內
晶圓廠客戶持續建廠，推升氟素內襯設備與管道系統的需求，年初在手訂單38
億元陸續轉為實質營收貢獻，且低毛利訂單已消化完畢，預期在高毛利訂單出
貨下，推估毛利率進⼀步提升1.38ppts至37%，稅後EPS 4.46元，2026全年營
收年增18%至55.8億元，毛利率提升3.6ppts至36.58%，稅後EPS 14.45元。 
擴產與高階新品推升獲利成⻑ ：2027年受惠兩岸產能擴充及高單價槽⾞ 、 建廠
設備進入交貨高峰 ， 2026年槽⾞接單量已超越去年的2倍突破1000台 ， 台灣廠
月產能將由80台提升至140台，並規劃進⼀步擴至170台，中國嘉興廠亦將提
升產能20%，預計自1Q27起挹注營收，產品方面，高溫環境用離型膜、高純
度配件及過濾產品將隨客戶認證逐步放量 ， 帶動高毛利產品比重提升 ， PTFE低
介電材料則仍處配方測試階段，具中⻑期成⻑潛⼒，受惠在地化供應、⼯程整
合服務及產能利用率提升 ， 預估2027年營收年增25%至69.7億元 ， 毛利率提升
2.92ppts至39.5% ，費用率因非大幅建廠 ， 維持在11.8% ， 稅後EPS 19.84元 。  
 投資風險 
客戶擴產進度延遲、高階新品開發進度不如預期、原料成本壓抑獲利表現。

| 公司簡介  
主要進口上游高階氟素樹脂 ， 如 PFA 、 PTFE 原料 ， 並自行加⼯為半成品的板材與管材 ， 以及製造終端高階等設備 ，
核心產品包括內襯設備(桶槽、反應釜)、內襯管道與管件、化學品運輸槽⾞以及⼯程服務等，這些產品主要應用於
先進半導體製程，包含 2nm 以下，與高純度電子級化學品，如硫酸、氫氟酸、光阻劑等的生產、儲存、輸送與廢
液回收系統，由於氟素材料具有極佳的耐酸鹼腐蝕與抗污染特性，設備能將金屬離子控制在 PPT 等級以下、顆粒
控制在 0.02 以下，為晶圓代⼯與特化廠維持極高良率與穩定品質的關鍵所在。 
截至 2Q26 產品別營收，內襯設備佔比 68.3%，為公司最核心的營收來源，包含特化廠與晶圓廠建廠所需的整套
重化線純化槽、儲罐、反應釜及槽⾞等設備，其他材料與半成品 16.7%，主要為第⼀階段加⼯的氟素板材與管材
半成品，多數留作自用，部分出口給海外(日本、歐洲)缺乏加⼯業者的獨立市場，內襯管道與管件 9.6%，用於連
結各機台設備與廠區內的化學品輸送管線系統，⼯程業務 5.4%，協助電子化學品廠內部的襯裡與管道施⼯，受惠
於市場技術人⼒缺乏，該業務毛利率較佳。 
公司在台灣彰濱設有主要生產基地與研發中心，並於中國浙江嘉興設有⼯廠及美國亞利桑那州設有子公司，截至
2Q26 地區別營收，台灣佔比 49.3%，為目前成⻑動能最強的區域，受惠於晶圓代⼯龍頭與特化廠強勁建廠需求，
台灣廠同時負責供應美洲及全球非中國市場、中國 44.8%，由中國廠獨立生產運作，專門供應中國內需半導體與
特化廠擴建需求、美國 3.4%，因當地晶圓廠建廠審核較慢且缺乏專業⼯程技術人⼒，推進時程較⻑，目前營收佔
比偏低、歐洲 1.5%、其他 1%。

| 營 運 概 況 
擺脫低谷舊單稀釋，產品組合高階化使毛利提升：1H26 合併營收 23.6 億元，YoY+4%，其中半導體產業佔營收
高達 91.3%，主要受惠全球晶圓代⼯龍頭與記憶體大廠積極擴產，現有特化廠產能無法滿足晶圓廠開出的需求，
帶動建廠與特化重化線設備強勁拉貨 ， 其餘為傳統石化產業占 6.4% ， 做為維持基礎⼯業需求的穩定貢獻 ， LCD 面
板與太陽能佔 1.2%，其他佔 1.1%，毛利率年減 1.7ppts 至 35.3%，下滑主因在於認列的營收中，相當大部分來
自 2025 年半導體與特化產業處於景氣低谷期時所接的舊訂單，因當時因市場需求薄弱、競爭較為激烈，產品平

### Page 3

3 
均毛利率較差且接單總量偏低，進而稀釋 1H26 整體毛利表現，其中 2Q26 單季毛利率季增 1.7ppts 至 35.6%，
顯示高毛利的訂單已逐漸回升，1H26 費用年增 25%至 3.25 億元，其中 2Q26 單季費用季增 4%至 1.65 億元，
主因包含公司為維持先進製程氟素設備的技術領先，研發支出佔營收比重較去年同期增加 1ppts，另外公司依據
會計準則提列了預期信用風險損失評估，約占費用的 2%，若扣除信用風險損失評估，實質營業費用率仍穩定維持
在 11-12%區間 ， 1H26 業外 0.44 億元 ， 主要來自利息收入增加 ， 以及美元與人⺠幣升值所帶來的匯兌收益 ， 2Q26
所得稅因集中認列未分配盈餘加徵 5%稅賦，有效稅率較 1Q26 拉升 7ppts 至 21.6%，稅後 EPS 5.63 元，3Q26
單季營收季增 22.5%至 16.0 億元，受惠於國內客戶持續建廠，年初在手訂單 38 億元轉為實質營收貢獻，且低毛
利訂單已消化完畢，推估毛利率進⼀步提升 1.38ppts 至 37.0%，稅後 EPS 4.46 元。

受惠全球 AI 市場需求與 ESG 回收政策，帶動氟素相關產品需求：截至八月底在手訂單已突破 75 億元，公司預估
2026 全年新接訂單將年增 91%，由於 AI 需求推升全球半導體晶圓廠展開大規模擴產，目前僅晶圓代⼯龍頭在台
灣與海外同時進行興建的晶圓廠就多達約 20 座，隨著客戶現場建廠與設備拉貨排程推進，主要電子級化學品相關
製造商均必須進行大規模擴產與建廠，另配合政府政策、ESG 環保法規，中鼎(9933)等大型⼯程統包商均跨入回
收領域，直接推升氟素內襯設備與管道系統的需求，化學品運輸槽⾞因應晶圓廠大量使用化學品，預估接單量將
突破 1,000 台，相較去年 600 台是翻倍成⻑，目前槽⾞設備的訂單交期已往後推三年陸續交貨，客戶嚴格把關進
程，確保原料與槽⾞交期不致拖累建廠進度，由於產出的大型槽體與設備體積龐大，產品製造完成入庫後，必須
待客戶建廠空間準備就緒並通知拉貨才能認列營收，因此單季成⻑趨勢比單月數據更為準確，預估 2026 全年營
收年增 18%至 55.8 億元，在成本與毛利率方面，上游氟素樹脂原料，如大金(Daikin)、科慕(Chemours)，全球出
現嚴重供不應求，交期⻑達 8 個月以上，供需缺口約在 5-10%，且面臨 PFAS 環保審查與附加稅費增加的壓⼒，
但公司為原料廠的⼀線直接採購客戶，享有優先供料保障，並具備從原始樹脂顆粒自製半成品(板材/管材)到終端
設備的能⼒，另外由於公司產品設備外殼高達 90%為白鐵(不鏽鋼)，透過高市佔率優勢，能將每⼀筆新接訂單動
態重新計價轉嫁給客戶來降低成本壓⼒，預期在高毛利的產品訂單陸續開出，使整體毛利率回升 3.6ppts 至
36.58%，稅前盈餘成⻑ 40%至 14.4 億元，稅後 EPS 14.45 元。

產能擴充，持續投入高階新產品開發：2027 年預期營收受惠高能見度槽⾞與建廠設備大批量交付認列，高單價
與高毛利設備將於明年進入出貨高峰，另外新擴充的產能部分，以槽⾞為單位，中國嘉興廠進行 20%的產能提
升、動線優化與組裝委外調整，台灣廠已從每月 80 台提升至 140 台，皆於 1Q27 開始貢獻，台灣廠預計明年規
劃再提升 20%至 170 台，有效紓解目前產能滿載的壓⼒，支撐明年營收大幅成⻑。 
公司持續投入新產品研發，其中離型膜應用於晶圓廠高溫區環境的關鍵新產品，已開始小量出貨，預計於 2026
年底完成送樣，明年貢獻營收，高純度配件與包材，包含關閉時僅留 90% 殘留的射出件接頭、100L ⼀體成型
槽⾞杯、新型取樣器、濾芯過濾產品等，隨著客戶認證通過，高毛利產品出貨比重將在明年進⼀步拉高，⼯程業
務高毛利貢獻持續挹注，主要因應特化廠建廠與回收系統需求，化學廠內部的管道與襯裡⼯程訂單持續增加，在
⼯程人⼒極度缺乏的市場環境下，公司內部⼯程團隊提供整合性服務，使⼯程業務維持高毛利率表現，持續挹注
明年獲利，由於同業競爭對手大多採取保守態度不敢大幅投資，公司在地化供應優勢與積極擴產與技術升級策
略，預估市佔率將持續攀升，有助於毛利率表現。 
另因應 AI 與高頻高速傳輸技術的突破，如 PTFE/氟素樹脂與銅箔結合的低介電常數與低介電損耗(Low Dk/Df)特
性需求迫切，公司與特定下游廠商合作，進行高黏性 PTFE 氟素基板材料與金屬銅箔結合的開發與測試，由於技
術非常高，氟素樹脂化學惰性極強，提升其高黏著性並與銅箔緊密貼合，同時保持極佳的高頻電性，屬於材料科
學上的高難度突破，目前開發進度尚未正式通過客戶驗證，仍處於配方調整測試階段，屬中⻑期高毛利產品線。

### Page 4

4 
預估 2027 全年成⻑ 25%至 69.7 億元，受惠兩岸新產能開出、高毛利槽⾞與高階新產品比重提升，毛利率提升
2.92ppts 至 39.5%，費用率因公司擴充產能主要仰賴人員技術培訓、廠區動線優化與前後段⼯序，非大幅興建
廠房，因此未來不會產生龐大折舊費用來墊高費用率，隨營業收入規模擴大而稀釋，預期維持在 11.8%，營益率
提升 3.6ppts 至 27.7%，稅前盈餘年成⻑ 40.8%至 20.2 億元，稅後 EPS 19.84 元。

研 究  報 告                
力成(6239.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
4Q26 營收維持高檔，關注高階封裝業務布局進度  
 投資評價與建議 
我們認為高階封裝業務進度如期，且為明年公司重點布局業務，整體狀況與原
先預期差異不大；我們給予公司18倍PE，為近期股價修正前之平均評價水準。 
 重點評論分析 
估4Q26營收維持高檔：當前公司封裝、測試產能利用率均達90%以上，推估
營收動能包含：(1)記憶體需求維持高檔，新業務TSV DDR5開始量產，另公司
持續因應記憶體報價上漲 ， 適時反映於封測報價 ； (2)邏輯業務中 ， FCBGA需求
維持穩健且公司將於本季導入更大尺寸封裝 ， 子公司超豐Flip Chip月產能於年
底擴充至2.3億顆，至於Tera Probe/Terapower除了有AI/MML需求支撐外，
⾞用需求回溫亦帶來成⻑動能。估4Q26營收季增5.8%至268億元，毛利率
24.1%，稅後EPS 3.8元。 
高階封裝業務逐步發酵、封測報價議價能力強勁支撐2027年成⻑動能 ：展望
2027年，基於：(1)DRAM市場供不應求至少延續至 2027年，機構估2027年
DRAM報價續漲但漲幅收斂，推估將持續支撐漲價成本之能力；(2)邏輯業務
中，封測產業產能利用率維持高檔，持續支撐公司議價能力，而公司高階封裝
比重成⻑帶動產品組合持續轉佳，如FOPLP明年有望開出3k月產能，子公司
Tera Probe/Terapower持續搶攻AI測試業務，另Terapower新竹新廠將於明
年貢獻營收。預估2027年營收年增22.5%至1,182.4億元，稅後EPS 18元。 
 投資風險 
FOPLP進展不如預期。 
主要財務數據及預估值

| 營運概況  
3Q26本業表現大致符合預期：力成3Q26營收季增9.6%至253.3億元，毛利率24%，稅後EPS 3.44元，其中本業表
現大致符合預期，營收動能來自於記憶體需求維持穩健，子公司超豐價格調漲和客⼾需求成⻑，T e r a  
Probe/Terapower持續受惠AI/MML、HPC封測訂單支撐，而獲利方面則有價格調漲、高產能利用率(力成3Q26封
裝、測試產能利用率分別為85~90%、85%)之挹注，帶動毛利率季增2.3ppts。 
估4Q26營收維持高檔：當前公司封裝、測試產能利用率均達90%以上，推估4Q26營收動能包含：(1)記憶體業務需
求維持高檔，其中企業級SSD持續受惠於AI伺服器需求，另TSV DDR5開始量產，估月營收貢獻約1~2億元，價格
方面，公司持續因應記憶體報價上漲並適時反映於封測報價；(2)邏輯業務中，FOPLP驗證進度如期推進，FCBGA
需求維持穩健且公司將於本季導入更大尺寸封裝(78mm*88mm)，子公司超豐Flip Chip月產能於年底擴充至2.3億
顆 ， 另將部分不具成本優勢之產品轉移至菲律賓廠量產 ， 至於Tera Probe/Terapower除了有AI/MML需求支撐外 ，
⾞用需求回溫亦帶來成⻑動能。我們估4Q26營收季增5.8%至268億元，毛利率24.1%，稅後EPS 3.8元。 
高階封裝業務逐步發酵、封測報價議價能力強勁支撐2027年成⻑動能：展望2027年，基於：(1)DRAM市場供不應
求至少延續至2027年，機構估2027年DRAM報價續漲但漲幅收斂，推估將持續支撐漲價成本之能力，至於NAND
市況據機構預估明年仍有個位數的缺口或趨於平衡，僅有企業級SSD需求仍維持強勁；(2)邏輯業務則為明年最大成
⻑動能，基於整體產業產能利用率維持高檔，支撐公司議價能力，加上高階封裝比重成⻑帶動產品組合持續轉佳，
包含FOPLP明年有望開出3k月產能，推估應用為AMD之電競晶片，子公司Tera Probe/Terapower除了既有業務持
續成⻑外，也積極搶攻美系客⼾下⼀代AI ASIC測試商機，另Terapower新竹新廠也將於明年開始貢獻營收(估月營
收貢獻約2~3億元)。預估2027年營收年增22.5%至1,182.4億元，毛利率24.5%，稅後EPS 18元。

주가 추이

3분기 영업 이 익 은 지상방산 매출 감소와 일 회성 비용 등의 영 향 으로 
기대치를 대폭 하회했습니다. 4분기 실적 회복 과 중 장기 실적 성장 대
비 저평가된 밸 류 에이 션 에 주목합 니 다. 
3Q26 Review: 영업이익 0.5조원으로 컨센서스 대폭 하회 
3분기 매출액은 6.1조원으로 전년대비 6.0% 감소했다. 항공우주 부문
과 연결자회사들 성장에도 불구하고 지상방산 매출이 전년대비 약 0.9
조원 감소한 영향이다. 지상방산 내수와 수출 매출은 각각 0.7조원, 0.6
조원으로 전년대비 28.6%, 50.4% 감소했다. 내수는 대전 사업장 생산 
중단, 수출은 4분기 납기 집중에 기인한다. 항공우주 매출은 군수사업 
물량 증가로 전년대비 9.3% 증가한 0.7조원을 기록했다. 연결 자회사
인 한화오션과 한화시스템 매출은 각각 3.4조원, 0.8조원으로 전년대비 
12.9%, 30.1% 증가했다. 영업이익은 0.5조원으로 전년대비 49.2% 감
소했으며 영업이익률은 7.4%로 6.3%P 하락했다. 한화오션과 한화시스
템의 이익 증가에도 지상방산 이익 감소와 필리조선소 적자 반영이 영
향을 미쳤다. 지상방산은 일회성 비용 약 460억원, 원화 강세, 매출 감
소에 따른 고정비 부담 증가가 복합적으로 작용했다. 세전이익은 한화
오션의 외화환산손실 영향으로 -0.2조원을 기록하며 적자전환했다.  
실적 회복과 저평가 매력에 주목할 시점 
4분기 영업이익은 1.7조원으로 추정하며 시장 기대치를 상회할 전망이
다. 지상방산 수출 인도 물량이 집중되는 영향이다. 2027년 예상 실적 
기준 P/E는 12.5배로 경쟁사 Rheinmetall(22.5배) 대비 낮다. 약 35조
원의 방산 수주잔고를 기반으로 최소 2029년까지 실적 성장이 예상되
는 만큼 저평가 매력이 높다. 신규 수주 시점과 실적에 대한 우려가 상
존하지만 현재 밸류에이션에는 상당 부분 반영된 것으로 판단한다. 미
국 중간선거 등 국방예산 관련 정책 불확실성과 환율 부담이 완화되면 
투자자들의 관심은 4분기 실적과 2027년 신규 수주로 이동할 전망이
다. 연말 주요국의 국방예산 확정은 신규 수주 시점에 대한 불확실성을 
낮추는 계기가 될 수 있다.  
투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 1,300,000원 유지 
글로벌 재무장 수요 확대에 따른 동사의 중장기 성장 전망은 여전히 
유효하다. 투자의견 BUY와 목표주가 1,300,000원을 유지한다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10 월 30 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (채운샘) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
한화에어로스페이스는 당사와 ‘독점규제 및 공정거래에 관한 법률’ 에 따른 계열회사 관계에 있는 법인입니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 6

한화에어로스페이스 (012450) [한화리서치]

6 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 30 日(金)

1/5 
AI バブル測定器としての鉱工業生産（26 年９月） 
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・筆者が日本経済と株式市場の行方を読む上で定点観測している日本の鉱工業生産は、2026年
９月に前月比＋2.2％となり、市場予想（同＋1.3％）を上回った。経産産業省の予測補正値
（同＋1.6％）に対しても上振れた。増産は３ヶ月ぶり。８月が速報段階の同▲1.7％から確
報で同▲2.4％へと下方修正されていたため、９月の増産で前月までの落ち込みを概ね取り戻
した格好となる。もっとも、生産水準は直近最高だった６月の104.6を上回るには至っておら
ず、数量ベースの生産活動は、経済産業省の判断通り「一進一退」となっている。

・日経平均株価は先行き12 ヶ月72,000円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPYは先行き12ヶ月 155円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を12月に 1.5％とした後、27年末までに2.25％とするだろう 
・FEDはFF金利を、年内は4.0％で据え置くだろう

鉱工業生産指数

### Page 2

2/5 
・増産となったのは８業種。上位３業種は自動車工業、生産用機械工業、汎用・業務機械工業
であった。反対に減産の寄与度が大きかった３業種は鉄鋼・非鉄金属、窯業・土石製品工
業、化学工業（除く無機・有機化学工業・医薬品）であった。７月に熊本で発生した地震に
よって８月までは自動車生産などに影響が出ていたが、その挽回生産が９月の数値を押し上
げる格好となった。

・10月初旬に実施された生産予測調査に基づけば、製造工業の生産計画は10月に前月比＋5.1％
となった後、11月は同＋0.8％と増産が見込まれている。経産省経済解析室が生産予測指数の
上振れバイアスを補正した10月の予測値は同＋3.9％とかなり強い。電子部品・デバイス工業
と生産用機械工業で大幅な増産が見込まれているほか、自動車の挽回生産も続く見込みであ
る。生産活動全体の方向感を決める輸送機械工業は９月（実績）に前月比＋5.5％となった
後、10月（計画）に同＋0.9％、11月に同＋4.3％となっている。日本や米国における自動車
販売台数が堅調に推移していることを踏まえると、基調的に弱含んでいく展開は想定しにく
い。また生産活動の先行指標として有用な製造業ＰＭＩが４月以降に有意な上昇を示してい
ることから判断すると、製造業全体としてはＡＩ・半導体需要が浸透する形で増勢が強まっ
ていくと期待される。10月製造業ＰＭＩは53.4と高水準を維持している。

・なお、鉱工業生産指数は生産の「数量」に重きを置いて生産高を計測する統計であり、「付
加価値」を直接捕捉する性格のものではない。例えば、高付加価値を狙った品質向上によっ
て生産数量・重量が減少する場合、統計上はそれを減産と見做し、付加価値を過小評価して
しまうこともある。したがって、鉱工業生産統計が示すほど日本経済（製造業）の付加価値

3/5 
創出力が停滞していない可能性を念頭に置く必要がある。事実、過去数年は付加価値の合計
である実質ＧＤＰと鉱工業生産指数の水準・方向感の乖離が大きくなっている。

・株式市場における重要度が高い電子部品・デバイス工業については、前月比＋3.6％の増産で
あった。地震の影響もあってか、ＩＣの増産に一服感がみられる一方、コンデンサや電子回
路基板などで増産の動きがみられる。生産用機械工業に分類される半導体製造装置は同＋
3.4％の増産となり、３ヶ月平均値でみても＋7.7％と力強い増産基調にある。生産予測調査
に目を向けると、電子部品・デバイス工業は10月に同＋16.6％、11月に同▲1.7％と均してみ
れば大幅な増産が計画されている。半導体製造装置が含まれる生産用機械工業は10月に同＋
15.5％と大幅増産となった後、11月は同▲5.7％と減産が見込まれているが、こちらも基調は
強い。個別企業の業績予想などから判断しても、先行きは半導体製造装置の増産がかなりの
高確度で期待できる。関連指標の機械受注統計における「電子計算機等」の受注額水準も鈍
化する気配に乏しい。この指標は日経平均株価との連動性がある。

4/5

・電子部品・デバイス工業の動向を仔細にみると、９月は出荷が前年比＋0.8％へと減速するな
か、在庫は同▲6.1％へとマイナス幅を拡大した。この結果、出荷・在庫バランス（両者の前
年比差分から算出）は＋6.9pとなり、３ヶ月平均でも＋7.0ptとはっきりとしたプラス領域を
維持した。引き続き、製品需給が引き締まる方向にあることが示唆されている。ここで電子
部品・デバイス工業の出荷・在庫バランスと、日経平均株価が長期的に連動性を有してきた
事実を再確認したい。株価指数において半導体を直接手掛ける企業の存在感は必ずしも大き
くはないものの、半導体製造装置、電子部品、化学品など「広義半導体」で見ればその存在
感は大きく、結果的に日本株全体のうねりを作り出す構図があると筆者は理解している。ち
なみに出荷在庫バランス株価は方向感こそ一致しているものの、両者のかい離は大きくなっ
ている。その要因として、数量ベースの出荷・在庫バランスには、メモリなど製品価格の高
騰が反映されないことが考えられる。

Document 264: AI“完美风暴”剧本：债市“吹响号角”，财报“雪上加霜”，中选“最后一击”？

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI“完美风暴”剧本：债市“吹响号角”，财报“雪上加霜”，中选“最后一击”？.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 5

### Page 1

AI 热潮遭遇债市、财报与政治⻛险三重夹击：融资⻔槛抬⾼， AI 云公司债券市场近乎关
闭；科技巨头资本开⽀激增、⾃由现⾦流恶化，股价承压；中期选举临近，数据中⼼反对
声浪或推动监管收紧。市场正从追逐算⼒转向拷问投资回报。
摘要：
美国 AI 扩张正在进⼊⼀个更严苛阶段：资本不再只为增⻓叙事买单，债市、财报和政治⻛险同时
逼近，投资者开始要求 AI 投资证明其回报能⼒。
贷款⼈和投资者正变得更挑剔，并要求更严格条款。即便是 SpaceX 和 Meta 等⼤型科技相关发⾏
⼈的债券也在⼤幅折价交易， CoreWeave ， Nebius 和 Nscale 等 AI 云公司近期实际上已被挡在债
券市场⻔外。
财报季未能缓和疑虑，反⽽放⼤了市场对 AI 资本开⽀和⾃由现⾦流的担忧。 Nasdaq 100 指数 10
⽉下跌 6.7% ，市值蒸发 2.7 万亿美元。 Alphabet 股价在财报后单⽇暴跌 13% ，创下该公司史上最
差单⽇表现。
政治⻛险也在升温。随着美国中期选举临近，数据中⼼建设引发的电价、环境和监管争议正在

进⼊市场定价。若⺠调和预测市场显⽰的⺠主党重新掌控国会前景兑现， AI ⾏业可能⾯临更密集
的调查、听证和监管议程。
债市先变脸： AI 融资进⼊买⽅市场
过去⼏年，美国 AI 热潮背后的公司轻松融资数千亿美元。现在，华尔街开始提⾼⻔槛。
投 资 者 担 ⼼ ⼤ 型数 据 中 ⼼ 项 ⽬ 可 能 因 基 层政 治 反 弹 ⽽ 延 误 ， 也 担 ⼼ 超 ⼤ 规 模 云 ⼚ 商 以 及
Anthropic ， OpenAI 等 AI 初创公司的利润⽆法如预期快速兑现。与此同时，股票和债券融资供应
激增，使投资者担忧对 AI 主题的⻛险敞⼝过⾼。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

这种转变已经反映在市场价格中。 AI 相关债券发⾏折价扩⼤，部分发⾏⼈融资窗⼝收窄。 Pimco
⾸席投资官 Dan Ivascyn 表⽰，贷款⼈要求企业证明投资能转化为⾜够收⼊的⻔槛正 “ 越来越
⾼ ” ， “ 薄弱环节会最先承压 ” 。
即便资⾦仍然存在，条款也更贵。 SpaceX 本周宣布完成 400 亿美元融资，⽤于购买 Nvidia 最新
芯⽚，这被称为 AI 芯⽚领域规模最⼤的融资。但纪录仅维持数⼩时， Broadcom 随后敲定 630 亿
美元交易，向 Anthropic 出租芯⽚。即便如此，据《华尔街⽇报》报道， Broadcom ⾸席执⾏官
Hock Tan 仍需在最后阶段亲⾃介⼊谈判，以安抚银⾏疑虑。
新云公司承压：⾼收益债窗⼝⼏近关闭
压⼒最先集中在所谓 neocloud 公司⾝上。这类公司拥有或租赁数据中⼼，部署 AI 服务器，并向
⼤型科技公司、交易公司和 AI 初创公司出售算⼒。它们借款规模⼤，对利率和融资条件变化尤其
敏感。
据 Bank of America 数据，美国企业债市场中 AI 相关发⾏规模在 2025 年为 3940 亿美元，今年截⾄

10 ⽉中旬已达 6550 亿美元。分析师预计，明年新增 AI 相关债务规模将接近 1 万亿美元。
但市场承接能⼒正在被测试。 CoreWeave 今年早些时候以 9.75% 票息发⾏的债券，⽬前⼆级市
场收益率已超过 15% 。这意味着短时间内借款成本显著上升。银⾏家近期警告称， neocloud 公
司的⾼收益债新发⾏市场基本关闭， 15% 以上收益率对借款⽅⽽⾔成本过⾼。
Nebius 是⼀个典型案例。该公司⾃ 2024 年以来向 Nvidia 和其他投资者出售约 27 亿美元股票，并
发⾏数⼗亿美元可转债⽀持 AI 扩张。但据《华尔街⽇报》援引知情⼈⼠报道， Nebius 曾考虑⾸
次发⾏⾼收益债，随后决定不推进。⾸席执⾏官 Arkady Volozh 上周在柏林⼀场会议上表⽰，⼀
些资本来源 “ 暂时关闭 ” ，公司正通过客⼾预付款和其他⼯具筹资。
股权市场也不轻松。 Lambda 在 IPO 前最后⼀轮融资中，将⽬标估值从 145 亿美元下调⾄ 130 亿美
元。 Nscale 原本考虑 11 ⽉上市，⽬前倾向推迟⾄ 12 ⽉。该公司还在与 Nvidia 和其他投资者谈

判，为 Anthropic 位于西弗吉尼亚州的 450 亿美元数据中⼼提供融资⽀持，但潜在⽀持⽅在市场转
向后要求更严格条款。
巨头也遇到集中度约束
⼤型超⼤规模云⼚商的信⽤质量远⾼于 neocloud 公司，但问题在于融资规模过⼤。
Meta ， Amazon ， Alphabet 和 Microsoft 拥有⾼信⽤评级，也有能⼒继续增加债务。但多家公司
今年已⼤量发⾏⾼等级债，并暗⽰未来仍将持续融资，这使投资公司、保险公司和养⽼⾦等传
统买⽅开始担⼼单⼀⾏业和单⼀主题集中度过⾼。
Ivascyn 表⽰，市场⾯临⼀个现实问题：投资者在⼀个名字或⼀个主题上究竟还能买多少。
这种压⼒可能解释了为何部分公司转向股权融资。 Meta 公布计划通过⼀系列股票发⾏最多筹资
975 亿美元。其证券⽂件提到， Alphabet ， SpaceX ， Anthropic 和 OpenAI 等公司的近期和未来融
资，可能对资本市场深度构成挑战。
Ben Connard ， Carnegie Investment Counsel ⾸席投资官表⽰， “ 每个⼈都在流动性枯竭前为建

设计划融资。 ” 他称，最终可能没有⾜够资⾦⽀持整个 AI 扩张，融资可能成为拖慢建设的关键瓶
颈。

### Page 3

财报雪上加霜：现⾦流成为市场新考题
如果债市是预警，财报季则让市场重新评估 AI 投资的回报周期。
⼤型 AI ⽀出⽅的云业务仍在增⻓，但投资者更关注数据中⼼建设成本对⾃由现⾦流的侵蚀。
Anthony Saglimbene ， Ameriprise ⾸席市场策略师表⽰，市场正在变得更有选择性，因为并⾮
每家超⼤规模云⼚商都会成为赢家，关键在于公司花了多少钱，获得多少回报，以及护城河有
多深。
Alphabet 成为最明显的压⼒点。公司财报显⽰核⼼搜索业务销售令⼈失望，且连续第⼆个季度
录得负⾃由现⾦流，这是其上市以来从未出现过的情况。搜索去年占 Alphabet 收⼊近 56% ，因
此 AI 搜索增⻓若伴随更低变现能⼒，将直接触及其核⼼商业模式。该股周四暴跌 13% ，公司市值
蒸发约 5000 亿美元。
Amazon 的报告同样未能安抚市场。由于资本开⽀上升且电商业务增速为三年来最慢， Amazon
负⾃由现⾦流显著差于分析师预期，股价周四和周五累计下跌 5.6% 。
Meta 的压⼒来⾃资本开⽀预期。公司 10 ⽉ 21 ⽇财报显⽰全年资本开⽀计划明显上升，此后股价

下跌 12% 。其信⽤⻛险指标升⾄纪录⾼位。 S&P Global Ratings 周五晚间将 Meta 评级展望从稳
定下调⾄负⾯，称尚不清楚 Meta ⽀出何时能带来超过成本增⻓的收⼊。
现⾦流分化正在成为估值分⽔岭。四家超⼤规模云⼚商中， Amazon ， Meta 和 Alphabet 当季均出
现⾃由现⾦流⾚字。 Amazon 负⾃由现⾦流接近 350 亿美元，⽽⼀年前为正 4.3 亿美元。 Microsoft
是例外，录得 186 亿美元正⾃由现⾦流，但其股价周五仍下跌 1.6% 。
软件板块也被拖⼊ AI 定价重估
AI 焦虑并不局限于巨头和基础设施公司。软件股同样受到冲击。
本财报季 S&P 500 软件公司中， 90% 利润超过预期，但只有 70% 收⼊超过预期，低于上⼀季
度。市场担⼼ Anthropic ， OpenAI 等公司的 AI 产品可能削弱传统软件公司的定价能⼒和利润率。
ServiceNow 发布令⼈失望的季度销售展望后，股价周四暴跌 31% ，创纪录最⼤跌幅。 Fortinet 下

调服务销售预期，引发⽹络安全股抛售。 Twilio 股价周五下跌 18% ，因其展望显⽰增⻓可能明显
放缓。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
Ben Connard 表⽰，市场对哪些软件公司能够在 AI 世界中⽣存 “ 完全不安 ” 。
中期选举临近：政治⻛险进⼊融资模型
第三重压⼒来⾃华盛顿。
Meta 在准备约 980 亿美元股票发⾏之际，在⽂件中提到贸易争端、监管挑战和 “ 公众情绪转变 ” 。
公司警告，新的税收或监管可能进⼀步增加资本⽀出和运营成本。 Anthropic 等公司的⽂件中也
出现类似⻛险披露。
特朗普政府迄今维持对 AI 较宽松的监管态度，强调⾃愿安全措施、公司主导测试和第三⽅审计，
并推动将 AI 重新包装为 “Super Intelligence”。特朗普还在⽩宫接待近⼆⼗名 AI ⾼管，包括
Anthropic ⾸席执⾏官 Dario Amodei ， OpenAI 总裁 Greg Brockman 和 Nvidia ⾸席执⾏官 Jensen

Huang ，并促成⼀项⾮约束性安全承诺。
但公众反弹正在扩⼤。 Washington Post-ABC News-Ipsos 本⽉发布的⺠调显⽰，约三分之⼆美
国⼈反对新建数据中⼼， 57% 的成年⼈认为 AI 对社会是坏事，约 67% 不认可特朗普处理 AI 问题的
⽅式。数据中⼼被视为推⾼电价的重要因素之⼀，也引发环境和劳动⼒冲击担忧。
如果⺠主党在中期选举后夺回国会两院， AI ⾏业可能⾯临更强监管。彭博报道， Hakeem
Jeffries 正在考虑是否成⽴专⻔ AI 委员会，该委员会可能调查并传唤相关公司，重点包括 AI 驱动
的⽹络攻击扩散，以及数据中⼼建设对电价的影响。
政策提案范围也在扩⼤。部分⺠主党⼈⽀持对前沿 AI 模型建⽴联邦监管框架，并预先排除部分州
级规则。两党参议员也在谈判 AI 安全框架。 Bernie Sanders 提出过全国性数据中⼼建设暂停，
Ted Lieu 则提出在模型中设置应对灾难性伤害的关闭机制。
投资者的主线：从 “ 建得快 ” 转向 “ 赚得到 ”

AI 需求仍然强劲，资本开⽀也仍在推进。 Meta 未来⽀出承诺升⾄ 8250 亿美元， Alphabet 未来⽀
出义务超过 1 万亿美元。 Microsoft 称，尽管其财年第⼀季度资本开⽀因瓶颈低于预期，但本财年
下半年⽀出将更⾼。
问题在于，市场不再只奖励⽀出规模。
Zacks Investment Management ⾸席市场策略师 Brian Mulberry 表⽰，投资者正在⽤资⾦投票，
重新定义 “ 质量 ” 。 Fifth Third Wealth Advisors ⾸席投资官 Chris Osmond 称，市场开始奖励能把
⽀出转化为收⼊和实际投资回报的公司，惩罚那些尚未证明这⼀点却继续增加⽀出的公司。
这意味着 AI 交易进⼊ “ 证明阶段 ” 。债市要求更⾼收益率和更强保护条款，股市要求更清晰现⾦流
路径，政治体系则可能要求更严格监管。对投资者⽽⾔， AI 主题没有消失，但筛选标准已经改
变：融资能⼒、⾃由现⾦流、资本开⽀纪律和监管暴露，正在取代单纯的增⻓叙事。

Document 349: India_ A middle class too small to supplant China’s economic role (2026-10-26)

Source PDF: all report pdf/India_ A middle class too small to supplant China’s economic role (2026-10-26).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
At a time when Chinese consumption is slowing significantly, could India become a driver of global consumption? For now, this
prospect presents considerable difficulties, with many obstacles to overcome. Despite dynamic growth and the Indian economy’s
expanding share of global output (8.2% of worldwide GDP in 2025), the middle-class consumer population remains modest and is nowhere
near the size of China’s.
This is due to the concentration of employment in low-value-added and largely informal sectors. The few manufacturing sectors with

high value-added and strong job potential — electrical equipment, electronics, automotive, chemicals and pharmaceuticals — continue to
face constraints from an unfavourable business environment and a skills shortage that is holding back their move into premium market
segments.
Furthermore, the highest-paying service sector jobs, particularly in information technology, are now threatened by the rise of
artificial intelligence. Without concrete measures, the “demographic window” — which is expected to remain favourable for another 15 to

25 years — could close before India establishes a solid industrial base and a sufficiently large middle class.
INDIA: A MIDDLE CLASS TOO SMALL TO SUPPLANT CHINA’S ECONOMIC ROLE
Johanna Melka
issue 20
26 October 2026

### Page 2

economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoInsight 20 / 26 October 2026

for a changing
worldINDIA: A MIDDLE CLASS TOO SMALL TO SUPPLANT CHINA’S ECONOMIC ROLE
INDIA IS STRUGGLING TO ESTABLISH A ROBUST MIDDLE
CLASS
With an annual growth rate of 7.3% over the past decade (excluding the
pandemic), India has one of the highest growth rates among the world’s
major economies. Yet, its middle-class consumer base remains surpri-
singly small. When measured in current U.S. dollars, total consumption

is very high in India (USD 2,241 billion in 2025), but this figure prima -
rily reflects the country’s overall population size. By comparison, this
consumption level falls 3.5 times short of China’s corresponding figure.
Furthermore, per capita consumption adjusted for purchasing power
parity (PPP) is 1.3 times lower than that of Indonesia, the Philippines
and Vietnam. When evaluated at constant prices, consumption per ca-
pita has increased only slightly over the past thirty years compared to

China and even Vietnam (Chart 1).
According to our estimates, based on the research conducted by Ló -
pez-Calva and Ortiz-Juarez (2014) 1, India’s middle-class consumer
segment accounts for just 1.6% of the total population (see Table 1),
or fewer than 24 million people. This is less than in Thailand and Viet-
nam (more than 30 million each) and far below China (approximately
490 million, according to the same definition).
The majority of households identified as middle class by standard In -

dian research institutions (the Centre for Monitoring Indian Economy
[CMIE] and the National Council of Applied Economic Research [NCAER])
would, in fact, be categorised as “vulnerable” according to World Bank
criteria: households that have officially emerged from poverty yet lack
the financial stability necessary for sustained economic security.
Highly concentrated consumption and limited
upward mobility
The structure of Indian household consumption reveals significant

disparities between urban and rural areas. In 2024, average month -
ly consumption per capita amounted to 6,996 rupees in urban areas
1 These authors define the middle class as individuals for whom the risk of falling back into poverty is limited (less than 4% over four years), corresponding to daily per capita income
ranging from 17 to 98 2021 PPP dollars (with vulnerability thresholds at 6.85 and 14 2017 PPP dollars, according to the World Bank). In the absence of reliable national income

data collection mechanisms in India, our study applies these thresholds to the World Bank’s consumer expenditure data, an approach aligned with the objective of assessing actual
purchasing capacity deployed in the market (although this tends to slightly underestimate the middle class in terms of income, as household savings remain unaccounted for in
expenditure measurements).
(approximately USD 84.5), compared with 4,122 rupees in rural areas
(less than USD 50). The wealthiest 5% of urban consumers spent an

average equivalent of USD 245 per month per person. Consequently,
discretionary spending power remained concentrated among the hi -
ghest income brackets.
The structure of consumption has changed over the past decade. The
proportion allocated to food purchases (34.8% of total spending on ave-
rage) declined by 4.3 percentage points between 2011–12 and 2023–
24, while allocations to transportation and communications increased
to 12.4% (+3.8 pp). Although the allocation to durable goods has in -

creased, it remains below 7% of total household spending. This increase
was driven more by a rise in the use of credit than by income growth.
Over the past ten years, consumer loans have been the main source of
household debt. Although still modest relative to GDP , household debt
rose by 12.2 pp to reach 47.8% of GDP by the end of 2025, according to
the Bank for International Settlements (BIS).

THE LABOUR MARKET SUFFERS FROM STRUCTURAL
WEAKNESSES
The limited size of the middle class and the low level of household
consumption in India can be attributed, in particular, to the enduring
structural weaknesses of the labour market. The labour market faces
two major problems: insufficient job creation and the fact that most of
the jobs created are low-skilled and low-paid.

Insufficient job creation
Despite the Indian economy’s robust growth, its employment rate re -
mains substantially lower than in comparable Asian economies: 52.7%
in 2025 according to the International Labour Organization (ILO), com-
pared with 65.6% in Indonesia and 71.3% in Vietnam. This disparity
is mainly due to low labour force participation among women (34%
compared with 53.4% and 68%) and young people (40.8% of those aged
20-24 compared with 66.7% and 69.4%).

Over the past ten years, employment growth has averaged just 2.4%
annually, whereas GDP growth was close to 6% per year.
The services sector (representing 33.2% of employment in 2025) ac -
counted for the bulk of job creation during this period (+3.2% per year),
far ahead of the manufacturing sector (+1.4%) and, above all, agricultu-
re (+0.1%), which nevertheless remains the country’s largest employer
(41.5% of the workforce). This is not enough to absorb the 10 to 12 mil-

lion new entrants to the labour market each year.
2 Employment elasticity remained relatively stable between 1990 and 2012.
A limited number of sectors have significant po-
tential for creating high-quality, well-paid jobs
To identify sectors with high potential for job creation, we calculate
employment elasticity both for aggregate economy (KLEMS data) and
for the formal manufacturing sector alone (Annual Survey of Industries,
2025).
Over the period 1981–2023, aggregate employment elasticity remained

low at a virtually unchanged level, close to 0.3. This stability, however,
masks significant sectoral divergences: elasticity remained stable in
the services sector throughout the period (0.5), but it declined shar -
ply in the manufacturing sector (from 0.4 during 1981–1990 to just
0.1 during 2012–2023 2): at the same growth rate, India now creates
far fewer industrial jobs per unit of growth than it did forty years ago
(Table 2). In the formal manufacturing sector alone, employment elas-

ticity reached 0.7 over the past ten years, driven by very robust job
creation. However, this positive trend has not been sufficient to offset
the decline in informal sector employment, which accounts for 73.4%
of total manufacturing employment but only 38% of the sector’s value
added. Labour productivity in the formal manufacturing sector is thus
estimated to be 5.4 times higher than in the informal sector.
In capital-intensive sectors, employment elasticity ranged from 0.5 to

0.6 (including electrical equipment, transportation, chemicals and me-
tals), whereas in labour-intensive sectors — specifically wood, textiles
and apparel, and manufacturing food — employment elasticities re -
mained particularly low, or in certain cases, negative.

world
In other words, growth in value added in these sectors with high labour 
absorption potential is primarily driven by productivity gains rather 
than job creation. Furthermore, even among companies operating in 
the formal sector, employment elasticity remains low at an average of 
0.4. Consequently, the traditional mechanism facilitating labour mobi-
lity from the agricultural sector to the labour-intensive manufacturing 
sector has been very limited over the past forty years.
The only manufacturing subsectors that combine strong growth, high 
employment elasticity, and high productivity — thereby offering real 
potential for creating high-quality jobs — are electronics and infor -
mation technology, electrical equipment, the automotive industry, the 
chemical industry, and, to a lesser extent, the pharmaceutical industry. 
In these subsectors, the formal employment elasticity ranges from 0.8 
to 1.2 (Chart 2). However, in 2025, these jobs accounted for just 1.4% 
of total employment and 5.9% of GDP . The potential therefore remains 
negligible at the macroeconomic level.
Several structural constraints limit job creation in 
India
- The high rigidity of the labour market: it consists mainly of small, in-
formal businesses — those with fewer than 50 employees account for 
65% of the productive sector — largely due to the stringency of labour 
legislation (for example, companies with more than 300 employees 
cannot carry out mass layoffs without authorisation).
- Numerous obstacles impede the growth of industrial and job-crea -
ting businesses: an unfavourable business environment (particularly 
difficulties in accessing land acquisition to build factories); high costs 
of accessing capital (particularly for small and medium-sized enter -
prises); insufficient export competitiveness in labour-intensive sectors 
(textiles, clothing), which encounter disadvantages, including less ad -
vantageous tariff conditions3 than competing Asian economies.
- The mismatch between the skills of the available workforce and the 
needs of businesses. This mismatch constrains wage growth and the 
development of household purchasing power, obstructs industrial ad -
vancement, and, consequently, the creation of high-quality jobs.
INADEQUATE JOB QUALITY DUE TO A LACK OF  
APPROPRIATE SKILLS
A predominantly informal and low-paid labour  
market
According to the ILO, 87.2% of total employment in India is informal, 
with 73.4% concentrated in the manufacturing sector alone, predomi -
nantly engaged in low-value-added activities that require few skills. 
Furthermore, the Periodic Labour Force Survey (PLFS) 2023/24 indi -
cates that 58.4% of Indian workers are self-employed, 19.8% are day 
labourers, and only 21.7% are regular employees — the highest-paying 
status, with a wage gap of more than 60% compared to the other two 
categories. According to the ILO, 20% of the population captures 79% of 
aggregate labour income. The agricultural sector, which still employs 
41.5% of the labour force while contributing less than 15% to GDP , is

3 Historically, tariff rates imposed on Indian products exported to Europe have been higher than those applied to certain other Asian countries (Bangladesh, Vietnam, Pakistan).
4 According to the World Bank and the NCAER, educational and training programmes are too theoretical and fail to develop cognitive and interpersonal skills. Vocational education 
and training are not designed to meet the needs of high-demand sectors. Collaborative relationships between educational institutions and employers — particularly regarding 
internships and on-the-job training — remain inadequately developed.
3.7 times less productive than the services sector, with a median hourly 
wage that is 40 to 50% lower.
A widespread skills shortage
This concentration of earnings reflects the low skill levels of the jobs 
held: according to the PLFS, 88.2% of workers hold jobs requiring little 
or no skill. The proportion of skilled jobs is just 11.2% and has declined 
by 4.6 pp since 2018. The underlying challenge is not a shortage of 
graduates but rather a misalignment between what the labour market 
needs and the educational preparation provided to graduates 4, which 
results in their gradual downward mobility into positions far below 
their formal qualifications.
Unemployment, the downward mobility of graduates, and a shortage 
of technical skills
This disparity between skills and business needs directly contributes to 
unemployment among graduates, which reached 13.5% in 2025 accor-
ding to the ILO, compared with 1.3% for individuals without a degree. 
Within the 25- to 34-year-old demographic, the unemployment rate 
stands at 15.2% for graduates (19.2% for those with a master’s de -
gree), compared with an average of 6.1% across this age group. Only 
8.3% of graduates secure positions that genuinely correspond to their 
educational attainment; 53% of bachelor’s degree holders and 36% of 
master’s degree holders are overqualified for their positions.
In low-skilled jobs, the lack of technical training represents a signifi -
cant barrier to youth employment: according to the World Bank’s En -
terprise Survey (2022), 17.8% of traditional service companies and 14% 
of “intermediate” manufacturing companies cite this as a major obsta-
cle. In India’s manufacturing sector, 32.9% of positions are reportedly 
held by underqualified workers, according to the ILO, compared with 
28.8% in Indonesia and just 16.5% in Vietnam.
A direct impediment to industrial advancement
A survey conducted by the Ministry of Skill Development & Entrepre -
neurship across seven key sectors — automotive, textiles, agriculture, 
livestock, IT, commerce and green energy — reveals that the pace of

world
workforce training development significantly lags behind industry re -
quirements. This forces companies to train a considerable number of
young graduates themselves before they are ready for the workforce.
According to the Telecom Talent in the 5G Era report by the Telecom
Sector Skill Council, only 40% of IT and telecommunications graduates
are actually immediately employable.

This disparity in skills particularly constrains the transition towards
higher-value-added manufacturing activities within the sectors iden -
tified above (automotive, electronics and chemicals). Their growth
trajectory depends on technological advancement — electric vehicles,
semiconductors, specialty chemicals — and therefore on intermediate
to advanced technical skills that are currently lacking. Should trai -
ning provision fail to align with industry demand, employment elasti -

city and growth in these sectors will stagnate or decline, undermining
the only source of high-quality job creation currently available — and
consequently, the Indian economy’s ability to expand its middle class.
These structural limitations affecting national industrial development
diminish its appeal to foreign investors, despite its size. The stock of
foreign investment in India stood at USD 560 billion in Q2 2026, repre-
senting just 15% of GDP (compared with 20% in China, more than 52%

in Vietnam, and around 67% of GDP in Thailand and Malaysia).
ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE: A NEW RISK TO JOB CREATION
Artificial intelligence (AI) may also limit the expansion of high-paid
employment opportunities in the service sector. Its deployment poses
a significant threat to employment in India, particularly in the hi -
ghest-paying service sectors — the same sectors that possessed si -
gnificant potential for expanding India’s middle-class consumer base.

Agriculture (41.5% of the labour force), construction (12%), and trade/
transportation (18%) remain largely immune to the direct replacement
of workers by AI, which reduces the overall macroeconomic scale of
the coming shock. The service sectors offering the most lucrative em -
ployment — IT, finance and insurance — account for just 1.3% and 1.2%
of total employment, yet these remain the primary targets of automa-
tion. In IT services companies, the adoption of AI is expected to reduce

the need for entry-level roles (quality assurance, software testing and
support roles) but increase demand for positions centred on platform
development and management oversight. Current sector-specific evi -
dence indicates a notable slowdown in job creation within IT services
but no widespread contraction. A study by the Indian Council for Re -
search on International Economic Relations (ICRIER) on the IT sector
confirms a decline in job creation for routine and entry-level roles.

While new professions are emerging such as data centre operators, AI
DevOps engineers who serve as intermediaries between software deve-
lopment and systems operations teams, and ethical AI specialists, the
volume of employment opportunities generated remains substantially
lower than the number of jobs at risk. Furthermore, the skill require -
ments for these newly created positions exceed those possessed by the
existing cohort of graduates.
REFORMS ARE A STEP IN THE RIGHT DIRECTION BUT DO

NOT GO FAR ENOUGH
Since 2019, the Modi government has implemented a series of mea -
sures to develop industry, attract foreign direct investment, and gain
greater integration in global trade (after decades of protectionist po -
licies).
Corporate subsidy programmes (Production-Linked Incentives) have
helped support the development of those sectors with the greatest
potential for job creation (electrical, electronics, chemicals, and auto -
motive). Similarly, labour market reform (which came into force in late

2025) may alleviate impediments to business expansion and the crea-
tion of formal jobs. However, its actual impact will depend on its adop-
tion by the states, which retain considerable discretionary autonomy
over labour law, and on its effective implementation on the ground.
The free trade agreement signed between India and the European
Union in late January 2026 (which is expected to take effect in 2027 at
the earliest) should mitigate the tariff disadvantage experienced by la-

bour-intensive industries: the EU has pledged to remove tariffs, which
currently range from 4% to 12% on textiles and clothing.
However, these measures alone will be inadequate for creating
high-quality jobs if the education system fails to meet the needs of
Indian businesses. It is therefore imperative that India continues to
improve its business environment and, even more importantly, reform
its education system to promote job creation in high-value-added and

well-paying sectors. Without concrete measures, the “demographic
window” — which is expected to remain favourable for another 15 to
25 years — could close before India establishes a robust industrial
foundation and a substantial middle class.

*****
While India’s economic growth has demonstrated considerable dy -
namism, this growth has not translated into the development of the
middle class. The real medium-term concern involves the emergence
of a “neo-precarious” middle class — substantial in terms of num -
bers but lacking the foundations necessary for advancing towards a
high-income economy (households with stable employment, the ability

to save, and social protection). The creation of formal, high-quality jobs
is currently inadequate to sustain industrial expansion and its progres-
sion towards higher-value segments. The cultivation of certain niche
activities, while promising, remains very limited and is far from benefi-
ting the entire population. Finally, the development of AI poses more of
a risk than a genuine opportunity for the country’s development. India
lacks both the infrastructure and the educational system necessary to

deploy AI in a way that would yield tangible benefits for its economy
and labour market.

Document 356: Is AI Useless_

Source PDF: all report pdf/Is AI Useless_.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
